5. māṇavarkaḷukku ācaṉam ceyya iṭam iruppatu pōla, vaṭṭa vaṭiva amaippil allatu arai vaṭṭa amaippil māṇavarkaḷai iruttiṭa vēṇṭum.

6. kaṟṟuttarap pōkiṟa ācaṉattiṉ peyarai mutalil kūṟi, ataṉ amaippu, ceytāl kiṭaikkum payaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟai viḷakki, ataṉai eṇṇikkai muṟaiyil eppaṭic ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatai, āciriyar ceyal viḷakkattuṭaṉ ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

piṟaku māṇavarkaḷai, ovvoru eṇṇikkaiyilum ceytiṭap paṇittu, kuṟaikaḷai nivarttittu, piṟaku toṭarcciyākac ceyyumāṟu kaṟṟuttara vēṇṭum.

﻿7. ācaṉattai ārampikkiṟapōtu mūcciḻuttu, muṭikkiṟapōtu mūccuviṭukiṟa muṟaiyil payiṟciyaḷikka vēṇṭum.

8. ācaṉam ceytu muṭikkiṟapōtu cavācaṉam eṉkiṟa ācaṉattai ceyyumāṟu aṟivuṟutti muṭittiṭa vēṇṭum.

9. ācaṉaṅkaḷ ceykiṟapōtu, āciriyar kaṭṭāyam arukē iruntu, kavaṉittu, kaṇkāṇittu, toṭara vēṇṭum. cūriya namaskāram eṉṟa ācaṉattai māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkalām.

kuṟippu : payaṉ tarum yōkācaṉap payiṟcikaḷ eṉṉum eṉ puttakattil virivāka eḻutiyirukkiṟēṉ. āciriyarkaḷ paṭittum paṭippittum payaṉ peṟumāṟu kēṭṭuk koḷkiṟēṉ.

3. aṇi naṭaip payiṟci (Marching)

aṇi naṭaip payiṟci, māṇava māṇaviyarkku mikavum avaciyamākum. uṭaṟkalvip pāṭattiṭṭattil, kaṭṭāyam cērkkappaṭa vēṇṭiya pāṭamākum.

oḻuṅku (Discipline); kaṭṭaḷaikkuk kīḻppa ṭital (obedience); tāḷalaya uṇarvu (Rhythm); nalla uṭal tōraṇai (Good posture), kuḻu uṇarvu (Group sense) mutaliya paṇpukaḷai aṇi naṭaip payiṟci māṇavarkku arumaiyāka vaḷarttu viṭukiṟatu.

aṇi naṭaip payiṟciyaik kaṟṟut tara, aṭippaṭai iyakkam paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷai, āciriyar viḷakkamāka mutalil kūṟa vēṇṭum.

kīḻkkāṇum kaṭṭaḷaikaḷai āciriyar kūṟa, māṇavarkaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum.

1. oru varicaiyil nil (Stand in a Single line)

iyalpāka nil (Stand at ease)

nērē ... nil (Atten ...tion)

ōyvāka nil (Stand - easy)

piṟaku, varicaiyil nērāka niṟkac ceyyum kaṭṭaḷaikaḷai āciriyar koṭukka vēṇṭum.

⁠valappuṟam ... caricey (Right...Dress)

⁠nērē ... pār (Atten...tion)

⁠iṭappuṟam ... pār (Left...Dress)

nērēpār eṉṟu aṟivippukkup piṟaku, 1, 2 eṉṟa eṇṇikkaiyil, māṇavarkaḷai tirumpac ceyya (Turn) tarukiṟa kaṭṭaḷaikaḷaik koṭukka vēṇṭum.

⁠valappuṟam ... tiruppu (Left...Turn)

⁠ iṭappuṟam ... pār (Left...Turn)

⁠piṉpuṟam ... pār (About...Turn)

ivvāṟu tirumpuvatai (Turn) iraṇṭu eṇṇikkaiyil māṇavarkaḷaic ceyyumāṟu kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

utāraṇamāka valappuṟam tirumpu eṉpataip pārppōm.

eṇṇikkai 1: valatu kutikāl mītum, iṭatu kāl viralkaḷ mītum kālkaḷai cuḻaṟṟi, valatu pakkamākat tirumpa vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikkai 2: iṭatu kālai valatu kāluṭaṉ koṇṭu vantu cērkka vēṇṭum. kālkaḷai cērttatum nimirntu, viṟaippāka niṟka vēṇṭum nilai naṭaip payiṟci (Mark Time)



nilai naṭai eṉpatu, niṉṟa iṭattilēyē kālkaḷai uyartti, iṟakki, naṭai pōṭukiṟa muṟaiyākum.

niṟkum iṭattilēyē naṭa (Mark Time) eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷaiyai āciriyar iṭa vēṇṭum.

varicaiyil viṟaippāka niṟkum māṇavarkaḷ, iṭatu kālāl toṭaṅkuvar. iṭatu kālai uyarttum pōtu, iṭam (Left) eṉṟum, iṭatu kālai taraiyil vaittu, valatu kālai uyarttum pōtu valam (Right) eṉṟum āciriyar collik koṇṭē irukka vēṇṭum.

naṭaiyai niṟuttu (Class ... halt) eṉṟatum, māṇa varkaḷ naṭaiyai niṟuttuvar.

iṭatu kāl taraikku varum pōtu naṭaiyai (Class) eṉṟum, valatu kāl taraikku varum pōtu niṟuttu (Halt) eṉṟum kūṟa vēṇṭum.

niṉṟa iṭattil naṭai pōṭak kaṟṟuk koṇṭa piṟaku, māṇavarkaḷukku, muṉṉōkki naṭa, iṭatu puṟam tirumpu, valatu puṟam tirumpu piṉ puṟam tirumpu eṉṟellām kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭa, māṇavarkaḷ naṭakkum pōtē, tirumpiṭak kaṟṟuk koḷvar.

iṭatu puṟam, valatu puṟam eṉpataṟku 1, 2 eṉṟa iraṇṭu eṇṇikkai eṇṇiṉāl pōtum.



piṉpuṟam tirumpu (About Turn) eṉṉum kaṭṭaḷaikkum 2 eṇṇikkai kūṟa vēṇṭum.

ovvoru muṟaiyākap pārppōm.

vakuppu māṇavarkaḷai valatu pakkam tirumpac ceyya, valap puṟam tirumpu eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷai iṭa vēṇṭum.

valatu kālai taraiyil vaikkum poḻutu kaṭṭaḷai yait toṭaṅki, iṭatu kāl taraikku varum pōtu, iraṇṭāvatu kaṭṭaḷaiyai muṭittu viṭa vēṇṭum.

piṉpuṟam tirumpu eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷaiyai, ceyalpaṭuttum muṟai

kaṭṭaḷai - 1. iṭatu kālai iṭatu puṟamāka taraiyōṭu tiruppi piṉpuṟamākak koṇṭu cellavum.

kaṭṭaḷai - 2. valatu kālai koṇṭu vantu, iṭatu kālōṭu cērttu viṭavum.

iṭap puṟam, valap puṟam, tirumpu eṉkiṟa pōtu, māṇavarkaḷ nērāka, viṟaippāka uṭalai vaittut tirumpuvar.

ciṟitu vaḷaivāka aṇiyai naṭakkac ceyya payaṉpaṭum kaṭṭaḷai Left...wheel Right wheel eṉpatākum.

iṭappuṟam vaḷaintu cel (Left wheel) eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷai koṭuttatum, muṉ varicaiyil mutalāvatāka iṭap puṟam iruppavar; metuvākat tirumpa, muṉ varicaiyil valappuṟattil mutalāvatāka iruppavar vēkamāka iṭap puṟam vantu, aṇiyiṉ varicai muṟaiyai cari ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.



tērcciyāṉa aṇinaṭai (Advanced Marching)

aṇi naṭaiyil mukkiyamāṉa mūṉṟu tirumpum muṟaikaḷaik kaṟṟuk koṇṭa piṟaku. māṇavarkaḷukku. tērcci mikkatāṉa, kāṇpataṟkuk kavarcciyāṉa aṇi naṭai muṟaikaḷaik kaṟṟut taralām.

1. iraṭṭai aṇi naṭai (Double Time Marching)

2. metu aṇi naṭai (Slow Marching)

3. etir naṭai or maṟu naṭai (Counter Marching)

4. kōla naṭai (Figure Marching)

mukkiya kuṟippu

1. metu naṭai pōṭum pōtu maṇikku 80 kālaṭikaḷ (Step) irukkalām.

2. vēkanaṭai pōṭum pōtu maṇikku 120 kālaṭikaḷ.

3. iraṭṭainaṭai naṭakkum pōtu maṇikku 186 kālaṭikaḷ eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭṭuk koḷḷalām.

viraivu allatu vēkanaṭaiyai niṟutta 2 eṇṇik kaikaḷ. iraṭṭai naṭaiyai niṟutta 4 eṇṇikkaikaḷil kaṭṭaḷai koṭuttu, niṟutta vēṇṭum.

4. ciṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Minor games)

culapamāṉa tiṟamaikaḷaiyum, eḷimaiyāṉa vitikaḷaiyum koṇṭatē ciṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷākum.

ciṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷukku, viḷaiyāṭṭu cātaṉaṅkaḷ atikam tēvaiyillai. kuṟainta iṭavacati, pōtum. nērakkaṭṭuppāṭum valiyuṟuttappaṭuvatillai. āṭṭakkārarkaḷiṉ kuṟippiṭṭa eṇṇikkaiyum kaṭṭāyamillai

nērattiṟku ēṟpa, vacatikku ēṟpa, paṅku peṟupavar kaḷukku ēṟpa, vayatu vittiyācattiṟku ēṟpa, viḷaiyāṭṭai amaittuk koṇṭu, makiḻcci poṅka āṭuvatu tāṉ, ciṟuviḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷiṉ ciṟapput taṉmaiyākum.

ciṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷiṉ pirivukaḷ

1. ōṭiviḷaiyāṭum āṭṭam (Running game)

2. ōṭittoṭum āṭṭam (Tag game)

3. cātāraṇa pantāṭṭam (Simple ball games)

4. toṭar viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Relays)

5. cākacac ceyalkaḷ (Stunts)

ciṟu viḷaiyāṭṭaik kaṟpikkum muṟaikaḷ

1. viḷaiyāṭṭukku ēṟpa, māṇavarkaḷai pirittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. cila māṇavarkaḷai āṭac colli, mātiri kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

3. māṇavarkaḷai tiṟamai vāriyāka kuḻuvākkiṭa vēṇṭum.

4. viḷaiyāṭṭil paṅku peṟa iyalātavarkaḷai, āṭṭattaik kaṇkāṇittu naṭattum poṟuppai aḷikka vēṇṭum.

5. paṅku peṟupavar ārvam kāṭṭi viḷaiyāṭukiṟa varaiyil, āṭṭattait toṭara vēṇṭum.

atāvatu, āṭṭattil cuvāraciyam kuṟaivataṟkuḷ. āṭṭattai niṟuttiviṭa vēṇṭum.

6. āṭṭam makiḻcciyaiyē aḷittālum, āṭṭattiṉ vitikaḷai yārum mīṟātapaṭi, kaṭṭuppāṭṭukkuḷ aṭaṅki āṭukiṟapaṭi pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

7. āciriyarum paṅkupeṟukiṟapōtu, māṇavarkku uṟcākamāka irukkum.

8. tōṟkiṟa kuḻuvait tēṟṟuvatum,veṟṟipeṟukiṟa kuḻuvai vāḻttuvatum āciriyar kaṭamaiyākum.

9. viḷaiyāṭṭait toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉpāka, iṭattai cari ceytu tēvaiyāṉa utavip poruṭkaḷai āciriyar cēmittu vaittiruppatu, ciṟappāṉa nirvākat tiṟku uttiravātamaḷikkum.

5 mutaṉmai viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Major games)

mikavum nuṇmaiyum, valimaiyum niṟainta tiṟamaikaḷ: virivupaṭuttappaṭṭu vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟa vitimuṟaikaḷ: atikamāṉa taramāṉa iṭavacati; tērcciyum ceḻumainiṟaintatumāṉa utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ; kuṟippiṭṭa nērak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ: ippaṭittāṉ āṭṭattai muṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭāya niyatikaḷ; ivvaḷavu āṭṭak kārarkaḷ irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa vitikaḷ koṇṭavaiyē. mutaṉmai viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟa uṇarvukaḷukkut tīṉipōṭuvatu pōla, kuḻuvākak kūṭi āṭum vāyppukkaḷai ivai vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

naramputtacaikaḷ oruṅkiṇaippu āṟṟal; vaḷarcci peṟukiṟa tiṟamaikaḷ; kiḷarcci koḷkiṟatiṟaṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷ talaimaip patavikkut takuti tarum cantarppaṅkaḷ ﻿viḷaiyāṭṭup peruntaṉmai mutaliyavaṟṟai vaḷarttu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

mutaṉmai viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷai, muḻumai pakuti muḻumai eṉṉum kaṟpikkum muṟaiyil kaṟpikka vēṇṭum

kaṟpikkum muṟai

viḷaiyāṭṭaik kaṟpikkum muṉṉatāka, ataṉ toṭakkam: toṭakkattiṟkāṉa kāraṇam; āṭukaḷattiṉ aḷavu; āṭṭakkārarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai; āṭṭattiṉ nōkkam, āṭṭattiṟkuriya utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai, āciriyar teḷivāka, curukkamāka viḷakkiṭa vēṇṭum.

māṇavarkaḷai viḷaiyāṭac ceykiṟa pōtu, ēṟpaṭukiṟa tavaṟukaḷait tirutta vēṇṭum. tēvaiyāṉa pōtu, vitimuṟaikaḷaiyum viḷakki, viḷaiyāṭṭukkāṉa aṭippaṭai tiṟamaikaḷaiyum kuṟippiṭṭuk kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

āciriyariṉ teḷivāṉa ceyal viḷakkam, māṇavarkkuk kaṟkiṉṟa ārvattai atikappaṭuttum.

māṇavarkaḷai pala ciṟu ciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirittu kaṟṟuk koḷḷa, payiṟci peṟa āciriyar utava vēṇṭum. ovvoru kuḻuviṟkum ceṉṟu, uṟcākap paṭutta vēṇṭum.

piṟaku, māṇavarkaḷai aḻaittu, mīṇṭum avarkaḷai kuḻuvākap pirittu, muḻu āṭṭattaiyum āṭa viṭa vēṇṭum. ﻿āṭṭattiṟkup piṟaku aṉaivarum oṉṟukūṭi, kalan turaiyāṭiṉāl, atu avarkaḷiṉ tiṟamai vaḷara utavum.

6. tāḷa laya ceyalkaḷ (payiṟcikaḷ)

naḷiṉamāṉa uṭal iyakka acaivukaḷai, naṭaṉam eṉpārkaḷ. ataṉaitāḷamum layamum nayamāka iṇaiyac ceytiṭa vēṇṭum. āṭiṭa vēṇṭum.

paratanāṭṭiyam, katakali, maṇippuri, kuccippuṭi pōṉṟavai uyartaramāṉa apinaya naṭaṉaṅkaḷākum. (Classical Dance)

kummi, kōlāṭṭam, pāṅkrā pōṉṟa naṭaṉaṅkaḷ kuḻuvāka āṭukiṟa kirāmiya naṭaṉaṅkaḷākum. (Folk dance)

uṭaṟ payiṟcikaḷai mikavum oyyāramāka kaṇkavar muṟaiyil āṭukiṟa jimṉāsṭiks, lecim pōṉṟavaikaḷyāvum, tāḷalayac ceyalkaḷukku utavuvaṉavākum.

tāḷalaya iyakkaṅkaḷai muṉṉēṟṟam tarum pakuti muṟai kaṟpikkum vaḻiyil (Progressive part method) kaṟpikkalām.

ovvoru iyakkattaiyum, ovvoru kālaṭiyākak (Step) kaṟpippatu nallatu.

mutal kālaṭiyaik kaṟpippatu (Step 1) piṟaku, iraṇṭāvatu kālaṭi iṭuvataik kaṟpippatu (Step 2). piṟaku mutaliraṇṭu kālaṭikaḷaik kaṟpittu, 3vatu kālaṭiyaik kaṟpippatu. ippaṭiyāka, ovvoṉṟaiyum taṉittaṉiyē teḷivāka, kūṭṭākak kaṟpittāl, ciṟanta payaṉai etirpārkkalām.

kaṟpikkum āciriyarkku cila kuṟippukkaḷ

1. kaṟpikkum payiṟciyai, muḻumaiyāka terintu koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum.

2. mutalil kālaṭi muṟaiyaik kaṟpittu, māṇavarkaḷ naṉku terintu koṇṭa piṟaku, kai acaivu muṟaiyaik kaṟpittu, piṟaku iraṇṭaiyum iṇaittu muḻutākak kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

3. māṇavarkaḷ kaṟṟu k koḷḷa kaṣṭappaṭukiṟa nērattil, āciriyar poṟumaiyāka, viṭāmuyaṟciyuṭaṉ kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

4. toṭarntu ceykiṟa muyaṟciyāl tāṉ, eḷimai yākak kaṟpikka muṭiyum. ākavē, eṇṇikkai muṟaiyil kaṟpittal nallatu.

5. eḷimaiyāṉa acaivukaḷiliruntu kaṣṭamāṉa acaivukku, māṇavarkaḷai uṟcākattuṭaṉ koṇṭu cella vēṇṭum.

7. jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷ

jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷai, cīruṭal payiṟcikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟalām.

tāṉē taṉatu uṭalai ciṟappākak kaiyāṇṭu, cākacam mikunta ceyalkaḷaic ceyvatu tāṉ cīruṭal payiṟcikaḷākum.

ittakaiya cīruṭal payiṟcikaḷ uṭalukku valimai, nalla tiṟamai uṟuppukkaḷ oruṅkiṇaintu ceyalpaṭukiṉṟa āṟṟal, tairiyam, taṉṉampikkai, puttikkūrmai, mūṉuṇarum muṉṉaṟivu; viḻippuṇarvu,cuṟucuṟuppu: tērnta nekiḻcci, turitamāka iyaṅkutal, pōṉṟa vallamaikaḷai vaḷarttu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷai 4 vakaiyākap pirikkalām



1. niṉṟu nilaiyākac ceyyum payiṟcikaḷ (Static





gymnastics)

2. Goog Gouéolo Luisb%hoir (Dynamic gymnastics)





3. Graf 35moor Luosi,956;t (Light gymnastics)





4. Gugyoutraor Luo Sir (Heavy gymnastics)





1. nilaiyākac ceyyum payiṟcikaḷ

kuḻuvā c cērntu, kōpurappayiṟcikaḷ (Pyramids) ceyvatai, itaṟku cāṉṟākak kūṟalām.

oruvar mēl oruvar niṉṟu allatu mutukiṉmēl amarntu, apapaṭiyē paṭippaṭiyāy iruntu, maṉitak kōpuram kaṭṭuvatu.

mikavum eccarikkaiyuṭaṉ, karuttāṉa kavaṉattuṭaṉ intap payiṟcikaḷaik kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

kaṟpikkum muṟaikaḷ

1. kōpurattiṉ aṭittaḷattil iruppavarkaḷ kaṉamāṉavarkaḷākavum; mēlē amarpavarkaḷ ilēcāka uḷḷavarkaḷākavum tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.

2. kaiyūṉṟi talaikīḻāka niṟṟal, talaiyil niṟṟal, vaḷaintu halācaṉam ceytal pōṉṟa tiṟamaikaḷ uḷḷa māṇavarkaḷākavum tērnteṭukkavum.

3. avacaram illāmal, nitāṉamāka, kavaṉattuṭaṉ māṇavarkaḷai ceyyac colla vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikkaiyil kōpurap payiṟcikaḷ ceyyum muṟai

eṇṇikkai 1 : payiṟci ceykiṟa māṇavarkaḷ, vara pōka vacatiyāka, mutalil varicaiyil niṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikkai 2 : vicilukkup piṟaku, aṭittaḷattil iruppavar, ataṟku mēl iruppavar. ataṟkum mēlē eṉṟu vantu, iruntu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikkai 3 : ellōrum vacatiyāka iruntapiṟaku, vicil oli kēṭṭatum, kōpura muḻu vaṭivaik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikkai 4 : vicilukkup piṟaku, kōpuram kaṭṭiya muṟaipōlavē, iṟaṅki kalaintu cella vēṇṭum.

kuṟippu : 1. vayatukkēṟṟavāṟu, iṉattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu (Sex) payiṟcikaḷait tērnteṭukkavēṇṭum.

2 mettaipōṉṟa virippukaḷai, pātukāppu nilaiḷaik kavaṉittu ceytu koḷḷavum.

2. viraiviyakka jimṉāsṭik payiṟcikaḷ (Dynamic)

kaṟpikkum muṟaikaḷ

1. tērnteṭukkum payiṟcikaḷ eḷitāka iruntu, piṉ kaṣṭamāṉatāka irukkumāṟu amaiya vēṇṭum.



2.āciriyar col viḷakkattuṭaṉ, ceyal viḷakkattaiyum muḻumaiyāka, cariyāka ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.



3. māṇavarkaḷ payiṟciyai ceykiṟa pōtu, āciriyar arukiruntu utava vēṇṭum. 4. māṇavarkaḷ piṟarai kalāṭṭā ceyyāmal, kēlipaṇṇik kuḻappāmal, vakuppai kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ nirvakikka vēṇṭum.

5. māṇavarkaḷai ciṟukuḻukkaḷākap pirittu, ovvoru kuḻuviṟkum oru talaivarai niyamittu, payiṟci ceyyumāṟu paṇikka vēṇṭum. āṉāl kaṣṭamāṉa payiṟciyiṉ pōtu, āciriyar avaciyam arukila irukka vēṇṭum.

6. jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷ ceykiṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷ paḻutillāmal irukkiṉṟaṉavā eṉṟu pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum

7. ellā māṇavarkkum vāyppu varuvatu pōl vakuppai nirvākittuk kaṟṟut taravēṇṭum.

8. enta nērattilum viḻutal, kai kāl piṭippukaḷ, pōṉṟavaikaḷ naṭakkak kūṭum eṉpatāl, mutalutavip peṭṭiyum avaciyam.

9. muṭiyāta māṇavarkaḷai kaṭṭāyap paṭuttuvatō, valintu ceyya vaṟpuṟuttuvatō, tavaṟāṉa aṇuku muṟaiyākum.

8. taṟkāppuk kalaikaḷ

tiṭīreṉṟu allatu tiṭṭamiṭap paṭṭu, taṉ mēl nikaḻappōkiṉṟa āpattukkaḷai, taṉṉampikkaiyāṉa āṟṟaluṭaṉ, taṭuttuk kāttuk koḷkiṉṟa tiṟamaimiku ceyalkaḷaiyē taṟkāppuk kalaikaḷ eṉṟu aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar. ﻿

ivai mukkiyamāka, 3 vakaippaṭum. pala nūṟu vakaikaḷ taṟkāppuk kalaikaḷāka aḻaikkappaṭṭālum, iṅkē nām 3 pirivukaḷ paṟṟiyē terintu koḷḷa irukkiṟōm.

1. kuttuccaṇṭai (Boxing)

2. malyuttam (wrestling)

3. kampuccaṇṭai (Lathi fight),



1. kuttuc caṇṭai

paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukkuk kuttuc caṇṭaiyaik kaṟṟuttara parinturai ceyyappaṭavillai eṉṟālum, kuttuc caṇṭait tiṟaṉkaḷai, avarkaḷ kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟa pōtu, vāḻvil pala cūḻnilaikaḷil veṟṟi pala peṟṟu vāḻvāṅku vāḻa utavum.

kuttuc caṇṭaiyāṉtu vīram, vivēkam, viḻippuṇarvu, cuṟucuṟuppu, taṉṉampikkai, nēram aṟintu nērttiyāka ceyalpaṭutal pōṉṟa kuṇaṅkaḷai vaḷarttu viṭukiṟatu.

kuttuc caṇṭaikku, niṟaiya neñcuram (Stamina) : uṭaluram, valimaiyākak kuttum tiṟamai mutaliyavai vēṇṭum.

kuttuc caṇṭaikkāṉa aṭippaṭaip payiṟcikaḷ. cālaiyil oṭip paḻakutal; kayiṟu tāṇṭik kutittal: kaḻuttu, aṭivayiṟu, kaikaḷ valimaiyākkuvataṟkāṉa payiṟcikaḷ mutaliyaṉa vēṇṭum.

kaṟpikkum muṟai

1. mutalil niṟkum nilaiyai (Stance) kaṟṟuttara vēṇṭum.

2. niṟkum nilai eṉpatu, uṭalai camanilaiyāka vaittuk koḷvatu.

3. kālkaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum, irupuṟamum pakka vāṭṭil viraintu iyaṅkuvatu pōl kālkaḷai iyalpāka vaittu niṉṟu: etiriyai oru kaiyāl kuttuvataṟkāka muṉpuṟam, maṟṟoru kaiyai etiriyiṉ tākkutalait taṭuttu koḷvatum pōṉṟa muṟaiyil vaittiruppatē, niṟkum nilaiyākum.

4. iyaṅkutal eṉpatu kālkaḷiṉ iyakkam (Footwork) kaikaḷiṉ iyakkam (Arm movement) ākum. kaicaḷum kālkaḷum cērntu tiṟamaiyāka iṇaintu ceyalpaṭumāṟu (Coordination) kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

5. taṉittaṉiyāka ovvoruvarukkum tākkum muṟai kaḷaiyum, taṭukkum tiṟaṉkaḷaiyum (Blow and Block) kaṟpitta piṟaku, jōṭi jōṭiyāka māṇavarkaḷaip pirittu, paḻakac ceyyutal

6. tākkum kalai, taṭukkum kalai iraṇṭilum tērcci peṟa nuṇukkaṅkaḷaik kaṟpittal.

7. naṉku payiṟci peṟṟavarkaḷai, pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟattakka nuṇukkattaiyum, tairiyattaiyum ūṭṭutal.

kuṟippu:

1.kaikaḷil tuṇiyāl kaṭṭuppōṭṭu kaiyuṟaikaḷ māṭṭāmal; vāyil taṭuppup poruḷ (Mouth Piece) vaikkāmal, caṇṭai ceyya aṉumatikkak kūṭātu.

2. mutalutavip peṭṭi vaittiruppatu mikavum avaciyam.

2. malyuttam

kuttuc caṇṭaiyaip pōlavē malyuttamum ciṟanta taṟkāppuk kalaiyākum. tairiyam, taṉṉampikkai, tākkum āṟṟalkaḷ: cantarppattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu putticālittaṉamāka iyaṅkutal, tacaikaḷiṉ valimai, uṟuppukkaḷiṉ uṉṉata valimai, ivaṟṟaiyellām malyuttam vaḷarkkum mēṉmaiyaip peṟṟirukkiṟatu.

itaṟkāṉa uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ cila. taṇṭāl, paskikaḷ, mallar kampap payiṟcikaḷ, kuṉintu eḻuntu uṭkārutal (Push-up and Press-up), cālai ōṭṭam, kayiṟu tāṇṭik kutittal, oḻuṅkumuṟaip payiṟcikaḷ ellām nalla payiṟcikaḷē.

tēvaikaḷ

1. malyuttam ceyya mettaikaḷai payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum.

2. viralkaḷil nakaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.

3. nakaikaḷ, aṇiyak kūṭātu. eṇṇey allatu vaḻukkum tiravam etaiyum mēṉiyil taṭaviyirukkak kūṭātu

4. mutalutavip peṭṭi mukkiyamāṉa tēvaiyākum

malyuttat tiṟaṉkaḷ

1. etiriyaip piṭittu vīḻttak kūṭiya vakaiyil niṟkum nilai. atāvatu enta nilaiyilum camanilai iḻantupōyviṭāta vaṇṇam, taraiyil uṟutiyāka niṟkum nilai.

2. etiriyiṉ kaikaḷai, kālkaḷaip piṭikkum muṟai (Holds)

3. etiriyiṉ mēl piṭi pōṭuvatupōla: avar pōṭukiṟa piṭikku etirppiṭi (Counter) pōṭutal.

4. etiriyiṉ piṭiyiliruntu viṭupaṭutal. (Escapes)

5. etiri timiṟi veḷivarātapaṭi, iṭukkip piṭi pōṭṭu, taraiyil aḻuttutal. (Pinning)

kaṟpikkum muṟai

āciriyar māṇavarkaḷukku, ovvoru tiṟamaiyāka mutalil viḷakki, ceyalmuṟaiyil ceytukāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

intak kalaiyil aṟimukamāṉa māṇavarkaḷai, ciṟuciṟu kuḻuvākap piritta pirivukku talaivarkaḷāka vaittuk kaṟṟuttarac ceyya vēṇṭum.

itaṟku pakuti kaṟpikkum muṟai payaṉpaṭum.

māṇavarkaḷkōpappaṭakkūṭiya cūḻnilai ēṟpaṭuvatai, āciriyar tavirkka vēṇṭum. taṭukka vēṇṭum.

malyuttap pōṭṭikkeṉṟu, cila muṉṉōṭip payiṟcikaḷ (Lead up Activity) uṇṭu.

a) kālāl pōṭum malyuttam (Leg Wrestling)

ā) kaittaṭi koṇṭu malyuttam (Wand Wrestling)

i) kaiyāl pōṭum malyuttam (Hand Wrestling)

iṉṉum, iraṭṭaiyar pōṭakkūṭiya ciṟuciṟu pōṭṭikaḷum uṇṭu.

avaṟṟai māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟpittu, malyuttattil ārvattaiyum tērcciyaiyum atikappaṭutti, āciriyar tiṟamaiyākak kaṟpittup payaṉ cērkkalām.

3. kampuc caṇṭai

kampuc caṇṭaiyai cilampāṭṭam eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ.

tairiyam, taṉṉampikkai, viḻippuṇarcci, vīramuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭuttal pōṉṟa iyaṟkaiyāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷai, merukēṟṟi vaḷarttuviṭukiṉṟa āṟṟal, cilampāṭṭattiṟku uṇṭu.

etirkāla vāḻkkaikku kampuc caṇṭai payaṉpaṭum eṉpatāl, paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukku, aṟimukappaṭuttuvatil tavaṟētum illai

vaḷaikōl pantāṭṭattil payaṉpaṭum kōlai, lāvakamāka iyakkuvatupōla, cilampaiyum lāvakamāka cuḻaṟṟum taṉmai eḷitāka māṇavarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭum.

itaṟku kāl iyakkam; kampuṭaṉ uḷḷa kai cuḻaṟṟal; ataṟkāṉa niṟkum nilai. ivaṟṟaik kaṟṟut tarukiṟa āciriyar, tavaṟukaḷ ceykiṟa māṇavarkaḷait tirutti, ūkkuvittuk kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

taṉit taṉiyāka ovvoru tiṟamaiyaiyum kaṟṟut tanta piṟaku, iruvar iruvarāka kampuc caṇṭai pōṭac ceyyalām.

tērnteṭukkum pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ iruvarum, cama aḷavil tiṟamaiyuḷḷavarkaḷāka iruppavarkaḷāka iruntāl, pōṭṭiyum naṉku amaiyum. āpattum atikam ēṟpaṭātu.

tākkavum, taṭukkavum kūṭiya nilaiyil kāyam ēṟpaṭalām. mutalutavip peṭṭi kaivacam vaittiruppatu nallatu.

9. oṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ

ovvoruvar uṭalilum niṟaintu ūṭāṭik koṇṭirukkiṉṟa iyaṟkaiyāṉa ceyalkaḷāṉa oṭutal, tāṇṭutal, eṟital pōṉṟavaṟṟai, vaḷarttu viṭavum, valimai koḷḷavum , mēṉmai peṟavum utavukiṉṟa muṟaikaḷē, oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.

oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷai mūṉṟu vakaiyākap pirittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. ovvoru pirivil uḷḷa nikaḻccikkum taṉit taṉiyēyāṉa tiṟaṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.

āciriyarkaḷ nikaḻccikkēṟṟa tiṟaṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷaiyum, kaṟṟuk koḷḷum māṇavarkaḷukkēṟṟa aṟivu nilai, āṟṟal nilai, eḻucci nilai, ēṟṟa nilai mutaliyavaṟṟai aṟintukoṇṭē, ataṟkēṟpa kaṟpikkavēṇṭum.

māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟṟuk koḷḷak kūṭiya vāyppukkaḷai niṟaiya nalki, avvappōtu ēṟpaṭum kuṟaikaḷait tirutti, avarkaḷukkut taṉṉampikkaiyai vaḷarttu, kaṟpittiṭa vēṇṭum.

cila kuṟippukkaḷ

1. uṭalai naṉku vēkamākavum, tīviramākavum oru nikaḻcciyil īṭupaṭuttuvatāl, māṇavarkaḷukku patap paṭuttum payiṟcikaḷai koṭukkātu, enta nikaḻcciyaiyum ceyyumāṟu kūṟak kūṭātu.

2. māṇavarkaḷukku āpattu ēṟpaṭā vaṇṇam, pātukāppākak kaṟpikka vēṇṭum (tāṇṭutal, eṟital tikaḻccikaḷ)

3. mutalutavip peṭṭiyait tayārāka vaittirukkavum.

paḻutu paṭāta cātaṉaṅkaḷākap pārttu, payaṉpaṭuttiṭa vēṇṭum.

iṉimēl, ovvoru nikaḻccikkum uriya mukkiya tiṟaṉkaḷ (Skills) eṉṉeṉṉa eṉpatai maṭṭum koṭuttuc celkiṟōm. āciriyarkaḷ mēlum viḷakkamāka avaṟṟaip paṭittu terintu koṇṭu pōtikkumāṟum kēṭṭuk koḷkiṟōm.

1. ōṭṭattiṟkāṉa kikaḻccikaḷ

oṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷai mūṉṟu nilaiyākap pirikkalām.

1. viraivōṭṭam (Sprint)

2. iṭai nilai ōṭṭam (Middle distance Race)

3. neṭuntūra oṭṭam (Long distance Race) virai vōṭṭattai ōṭat toṭaṅkupavarkaḷ, mūṉṟu vakaiyāka amarntiruntu ōṭat toṭaṅkuvar. anta ōṭṭat toṭakkattiṟkuriya peyarkaḷ

a) kuvi nilait toṭakkam (Bunch Start)

ā) iṭai nilait toṭakkam (Medium Start)

i) nīḷ nilait toṭakkam (Long Start)

inta mūṉṟu nilaikaḷilum, evvāṟu amarvatu eṉpatai āciriyar, māṇavarkaḷukku viḷakka vēṇṭum.

ōṭa utavum cātaṉattil (Starting Block) eppaṭi amarvatu eṉpataiyum payiṟciyaḷikka vēṇṭum.

ōṭa viṭupavar (Starter) eppaṭi ōṭa viṭuvār? avaratu kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ eppaṭi eḻum eṉpataiyum viḷakka vēṇṭum.

uṅkaḷiṭattil uṭkāruṅkaḷ (on your marks)

tayārāka iruṅkaḷ (Set)

ōṭuṅkaḷ allatu tuppākki oli (Go or Gun)

eppaṭi ōṭa vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum āciriyar viḷakka vēṇṭum.

mūṉ pātaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē taraiyil paṭa, kaikaḷ iraṇṭaiyum muṉṉum piṉṉum viraivāka vīci ōṭa vēṇṭum.

ōṭṭat toṭakkattil irunta vēkattai viṭa, ōṭṭa ellaiyai neruṅka neruṅka, atika vēkattuṭaṉ pāyntōṭa vēṇṭum.

ellaik kōṭṭaik kuṟikkum nāṭāvai mutalil toṭa muyaṟcikka vēṇṭum eṉpataiyellām viḷakkik kūṟa vēṇṭum.

nīṇṭatūra ōṭṭattiṉ toṭakkattiṟku,

uṅkaḷiṭattil nalluṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyavuṭaṉ tuppākki oli kiḷampum. uṭaṉē ōṭat toṭaṅka vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum māṇavarkaḷukkuk kūṟa vēṇṭum.

iṉi tāṇṭum nikaḻccikaḷaip pārppōm

tāṇṭukiṉṟa nikaḻcciyiṉ mukkiyamāṉa pakutikaḷai maṭṭum iṅkē kūṟiyirukkiṟōm. avaṟṟai evvāṟu ceyal paṭutta vēṇṭum eṉpatai āciriyar viḷakkuvatu mukkiyam eṉpataiyum iṅkē kuṟippiṭukiṟōm,

kuṟippu mēlum viḷakkamāka terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭu meṉṟāl nāṉ eḻutiyirukkum "nīṅkaḷum olimpik vīrarākalām eṉṟa puttakattaip paṭittu payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷavum.

1. nīḷam tāṇṭal (Broad jump)

tāṇṭuvataṟku muṉ, tēvaiyāṉa patappaṭuttum paciṟcikaḷaic ceyyac collavum.

tāṇṭuvataṟkuṟiya valimaiyāṉa kāl (Strong leg) etu eṉpataik kaṇṭaṟiya, māṇavarkaḷai pala muṟai ōṭi vantu tāṇṭac ceytu, kaṇṭupiṭittiṭac ceyyavum.

metuvāka mutalil ōṭi vantu tāṇṭac ceyyavum, maṇaṟparappaip pārttē tāṇṭāmal, mēlē pārttu tāṇṭac collavum.

uyaramāka 8 aṭikkuḷḷāka oru palūṉaik kaṭṭi viṭṭu, ōṭi vantu tuḷḷi tāṇṭukiṟapōtu, talaiyil palūṉai iṭippatu pōla uyaramākat tāvik kutikkac ceyyavum. (Heading the baloon)

tāṇṭi maṇaṟ parappil kutippataṟku muṉ, kāṟṟil naṭappatu pōla, kālkaḷai muṉpuṟamāka nīṭṭik kāluṉṟu māṟu paḻakkavum (Hitch Kick)

nāṉku pirivukaḷ itil uṇṭu.

1. utaitteḻum palakai varai ōṭi varutal (Approach)

2. palakaiyil utaitteḻal (Take-off)

3. kāṟṟil naṭattal (Walking in the air)

4. maṇaṟparappil kālūṉṟal (Landing)

aṇuku muṟaikku 16 tappaṭi kālaṭi muṟai (16 Steps) allatu 20 tappaṭi kālaṭi muṟaiyaik kaṟpikkavum,

2. mummuṟait tāṇṭa l (Triple Jump)

poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷaic ceytu muṭitta piṟaku, niṉṟu koṇṭē mummuṟait tāṇṭum payiṟciyait taravum. (Standing Triple Jamp).

itaṟku nīḷattāṇṭal pōla ōṭi vantu, valiya kālāl tāṇṭi atē kālil taraiyil ūṉṟi, (Hop) aṭutta kālil oru tappaṭi vaittu, piṟaku, (Step maṇaṟ parappil iru kālkaḷālum kālūṉṟutal (Jump).

inta muṟaiyai ōṭi vantu ceyyac collavum.



tāvi tappaṭi vaittut tāṇṭal eṉpatu mūṉṟu muṟai tāṇṭuvatu pōla amaivatāl, atai 3 : 2 : 3 eṉṟa vikitattil, ōṭivarum parappil kuṟittu vaittut tāṇṭip paḻakkavum.

piṟaku maṇalil kālūṉṟum muṟaiyaik kaṟṟut taravum.

utaitteḻum palakaiyai nōkki varukiṟa cariyāṉa aṇuku muṟaiyai, uriya vaḻiyil paḻakkavum. (Check Mark)

3. uyaram tāṇṭal (High Jump)

uyaram tāṇṭalil 4 vakaikaḷ uṇṭu.

1. kattarikkōl tāṇṭu muṟai (Scissor Cut)

2. mēṟkattiya uruḷal tāṇṭu muṟai (Western Roll)

3. kuṟukkuk kampu mēl uruḷal muṟai (Belly Roll)

4. pāspari tāṇṭu muṟai (Fosbury Flop)

poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷai ceyya vēṇṭum.

kālkaḷai taraiyil vēkamāka utaittut tuḷḷit tāṇṭi maṇaṟparappil kutikkavum. intap payiṟciyai kuṟukkuk kampam (Crossbar) illāmalē paḻakavum.

inta muṟaiyil, taraiyil utaittu eḻukiṉṟa kālālēyē (Take off leg) maṇaṟparappil ūṉṟa vēṇṭum. piṉṉāl varum aṭutta kālai (Rear leg), naṉku uyartti, uyaramākak koṇṭu varumāṟu paḻakavum.

kuṟukkuk kampam illāmal paḻakiyatu pōla, kuṟukkuk kampattai iṭatupuṟam uyartti, tāṇṭukiṟa pakkam taraiyil iruppatu pōla vaittu, tāṇṭac collavum.



iṭatu kālāl tāṇṭuvōrukku valatu puṟattil kuṟukkuk kampam taraiyilum, valatu kālāl tāṇṭu vōrukku valatu puṟattil taraiyil iruppatu pōl vaittut tāṇṭac ceyyavum.

kampu mēl uruṇṭu tāṇṭum muṟaiyai, 30 allatu 40 ṭikiri kōṇa aḷavil ōṭi vantu tāṇṭumāṟu kaṟpikkavum. 5 allatu 7 tappaṭikaḷil viraivāka ōṭi vantu tāṇṭukiṟa uttiyai uṇarttavum. itaṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷak kuṟippai (Check Mark) cariyāka amaittuk koḷḷak kaṟpikkavum.

4. kōlūṉṟit tāṇṭal

poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷait taravum.

tāṇṭavarukiṟavar iṭatu kaip paḻakkamuḷḷavarā allatu valatukai paḻakkam uḷḷavarā eṉpatai mutalil kaṇṭaṟiyavum.

tāṇṭa utavukiṉṟa kōlai, eppaṭip piṭippatu eṉpataik kaṟṟut taravum. valatukaip paḻakkam uḷḷavarukku kaippiṭippil iṭatukaikku mēlāka valatukai iruppatu pōl piṭikkac ceyyavum, iṭatu kaiyarukku valatukaikku mēlāka iṭatukai irukka vēṇṭum.

kōluṭaṉ cīrāṉa vēkattuṭaṉ oṭi varukiṉṟa muṟaiyaik kaṟṟut taravum.

kōluṭaṉ ōṭi varukiṉṟavar. ōṭi varukiṉṟa ticaiyai nōkki, tōḷ aḷavu cariyāṉa kōṇattil (Right Angle) amaintiruppatu pōla, ēntik koṇṭu varavēṇṭum.





ēnti varum kōlāṉatu, muṉṉum piṉṉum pōyvarāmal, piṭittuk koḷvatu nallatu.

kōluṭaṉ ōṭi vantu, kōlai muṉpuṟamuḷḷa kōlūṉṟum peṭṭiyil cariyāka ūṉṟukiṉṟa muṟaikku, niṟaiya paḻakkam tēvai.

taraiyil iruntē kōlai ūṉṟi, oru kālāl taraiyai utaiktut tuḷḷi, kōluṭaṉē uṭalai oṭṭiyavāṟu uyarē tākkit tāṇṭa muyaṟcippatait tavaṟiṉṟic ceytiṭap paḻakkavum.

ippaṭip paḻaki, kuṟukkuk kampattaik kaṭakkiṟapōtu mēlē celvataṟkum, talaikīḻāka niṟpatu pōla, kampiṉ, piṭiyiliruntu, kālkaḷai mēlē uyartti, kampukku mēlāka uṭampai vaḷaittuk kutippatum oru ariya kalaiyākum.

kuṟainta uyarattil kuṟukkuk kampattai vaittu, pala muṟai kōluṭaṉ ōṭi vantu, peṭṭiyil kōlai ūṉṟi, oru kālāla tuḷḷit tāṇṭi, uyarntu ceṉṟu. ilāvakamāka kuṟukkuk kampattaik kaṭakkum muṟaiyai ovvoṉṟākak kaṟṟut taravum.

cariyāṉa aṭaiyāḷak kuṟippai amaittuttara āciriyar utava vēṇṭum.

eṟiyum nikaḻccikaḷ (Throws)

1. kuṇṭu eṟital (Shot put)

poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ tarutal.

irumpuk kuṇṭiṉai eppaṭi kaiyil eṭuppatu; eppaṭi piṭippatu (Hold) eṉpataik kaṟṟut tarutal.





irumpuk kuṇṭiṉai niṉṟu koṇṭu eṟivataṟkup payiṟci aḷittal.

eṭai kuṟainta irumpuk kuṇṭaip payaṉpaṭutti, eṟiyap paḻakumāṟu vāyppukaḷaik koṭuttal. itaṉāl, eṟiyum māṇavarkaḷukku, maṉattempiṉai vaḷarttiṭa muṭiyum.

piṟaku, kaiyil vaittirukkum kuṇṭiṉai, eppaṭi viralkaḷāl taḷḷiṭa vēṇṭum? (Finger Flip) eṉpatai viḷakki, eṟiyumāṟu paḻakkutal.

niṉṟu koṇṭē, irumpuk kuṇṭai viralkaḷāl taḷḷi, uyarē, uyaramāka eṟintu paḻakutal.

ataṟkāka, atāvatu eṟiyataṟku muṉ niṟkum nilai (Stance) paḻakutal. uṭal caktiyai muḻuvatum, irumpuk kuṇṭiṉai eṟiyum kaikkuk koṇṭu varap payiṟciyaḷittal.

kuṇṭai eṟinta piṟaku, uṭal camanilaiyai iḻakkāmal vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷē niṉṟiṭap payiṟciyaḷittal.

ippaṭi ovvoru tiṟaṉaiyum kaṟṟut tantu, piṟaku mottamāka aṉaittut tiṟaṉaiyum ceyya vaittal.

2. taṭṭeṟital (Discus Throw)

patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷai aḷittal.

taṭṭiṉai, evvāṟu viralkaḷāl paṟṟip piṭippatu eṉṟa muṟaiyai viḷakkutal.

mutaṟkaṭṭamāka, eṟitaṭṭiṉai, viral caktiyaip payaṉ paṭutti, tarai mītu ōṭuvatupōla, uruṭṭi viṭutal. ataṟkup payaṉpaṭum cuṭṭuviralaip payaṉpaṭuttum nuṇukkattai viḷakkutal.

aṭuttu, taraikkum mēlē antarattil (Air), taṭṭu cuḻaṉṟu pōvatu pōla, cuṭṭu viralāl cuḻaṟṟi eṟiyac ceytal.

piṟaku, niṉṟu koṇṭē taṭṭeṟiyac ceytal.

ataṟkup piṟaku, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷēyē cuṟṟukiṟa ārampac cuḻaṟcikaḷai (Priliminary Swings), taṭṭu illāmal cuṟṟiṭak kaṟṟut tarutal.

ippaṭi cuḻalak kaṟṟut tanta piṟaku, taṭṭuṭaṉ cuṟṟi, taṭṭai vīci eṟiyak kaṟṟut tarutal.

taṭṭāṉatu, kaṭikāram cuṟṟuvatu pōṉṟa ticaiyil, (Clock wise) cuḻalum muṟaiyil cuḻaṉṟu pōvatu pōla eṟiya vaittal.

ippaṭi ellā aṭippaṭait tiṟaṉkaḷaiyum māṇavar kaḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭa piṟaku, mottamāka aṉaittut tiṟaṉkaḷaiyum cērttu, taṭṭeṟiyac ceytal.

3 vēleṟital (Javelin throw)

tēvaiyāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷait tarutal.

vēliṉai, eppaṭip piṭippatu eṉkiṟa piṭimuṟaiyai (Grip) mutalil kaṟṟut tarutal.

tōḷukku mēlē, vēliṉai evvāṟu piṭittuk koṇṭiruppatu eṉpataiyum collit tarutal.

vēliṉaip piṭittirukkum kai etu eṉpatai tīrmāṉitta piṟaku, vēl illāmal, veṟum kai koṇṭu. uṭalait tiruppi (Turn) eppaṭi eṟivatu eṉpataik kaṟṟut tarutal

piṟaku, vēluṭaṉ niṉṟu koṇṭu eṟital.

ōṭivantu, vēleṟikiṟa vitattaik kaṟṟut tarutal.

eṟikiṟa nērattil. kuṟukkuttappaṭi (Cross step) vaikkum muṟaiyai nuṇukkamākak kaṟṟut tarutal.

ataṟkāṉa, aṭaiyāḷak kuṟikaḷaiyum (Check marks) kuṟittu, niṟaiya muṟai, ōṭivantu eṟiyumāṟu ceytal.

caṅkilik kuṇṭu vīcutal (Hammer throw)

poruttamāṉa uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷait tarutal.

caṅkilik kuṇṭiṉai eppaṭip piṭippatu, evvāṟu niṟpatu eṉpatai collit tarutal.

caṅkiliyaip piṭittapaṭi, eppaṭi cuḻaṟṟuvatu eṉkiṟa ārampac cuṟṟu muṟaiyaik kaṟṟut tarutal.

ārampac cuḻaṟcikkāka, eṭai kuṟainta kuṇṭukaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.

taṭṭu eṟivatu pōla, itaṟkum kāl tappaṭiyum cuḻalum muṟaiyum (Foot work) uṇṭu. ataṉai, ovvoru kālaṭiyākak kaṟṟuttaravum.

mutalil oru cuṟṟu (One turn) piṟaku eṟital.



piṟaku iraṇṭu cuṟṟuc cuṟṟi eṟital.

piṟaku, mūṉṟu cuṟṟukkaḷaic cuṟṟi eṟital.

ippaṭiyāka, muḻumaiyāka eṟiyap payiṟciyaḷittal.

ituvarai, ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷaik kaṟṟut tarukiṟa muṟaikaḷaiyellām kaṇṭaṟintu koṇṭōm.

10 . nīccal pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ

nīccal eṉpatu niṟainta payaṉkaḷai nalkukiṉṟa nallatoru uṭaṟpayiṟciyākum. uṭal uṟuppukkaḷ aṉaittaiyum iyakkukiṉṟa aḷavil amaintiruppatuṭaṉ, ciṟanta poḻutu pōkku amcamākavum, makiḻccitarukiṉṟa campavamākavum nīccal irukkiṟatu.

māṇavarkaḷukku eḷitāka nīntum kalaiyaik kaṟṟut taralām eṉṟālum, apāyam niṟaintuḷḷa itaṉai, caṟṟu eccarikkaiyuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭṭāl, ciramamiṉṟi cantōṣamākkik koḷḷa muṭiyum

mukkiyamāṉa pātukāppuk kuṟippukaḷ.

1. āḻamillāta iṭattil taṇṇīr kuṟaintuḷḷa iṭattil, nīccal payiṟciyait toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.

2. tūymaiyāṉa taṇṇīr uḷḷa kuḷattil, nīccal kaṟṟut taruvatu nallatu.

3. mitakkiṉṟa rappar vaḷaiyaṅkaḷ, kayiṟukaḷ, kiṭaippatāka iruntāl, payiṟcikkup payaṉ paṭuttik koḷḷalām.

4. mutalutavi cātaṉap peṭṭi kūṭavē iruppatu nallatu.

5. tōl viyāti maṟṟum, nōyāḷikaḷ nīccal kuḷattil iṟaṅkāmal, pārttuk koḷḷavum.

6. māṇavarkaḷ taṇṇīril iṟaṅka, kuḷikka, kutittu viḷaiyāṭa ārvamākat tāṉ iruppārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ ācaikkāka, appaṭiyē viṭṭu viṭuvatu, āpattāṉa viṣayamākum.

ākavē, koñcaṅ koñcamāka, avarkaḷukku intak kalaiyaik kaṟṟut tara vēṇṭum.

1. taṇṇīril niṉṟu koṇṭu, kaḻuttaḷavu iruntu taṇṇīraip payamiṉṟi cantikkum tuṇivai vaḷarttal.

2. taṇṇīril mitakkum kalaiyaik kaṟṟut tarutal. (Water Balance)

3. taṇṇīrukku mēlē mitantu, muṉṉēṟac ceytal.

4. taṇṇīrukkuḷ mūccaṭakkik koḷkiṟa payiṟciyai aḷittal.

5. piṟaku, taṇṇīril ūrntu celkiṟa nīccal payiṟciyai (Crawl stroke) kaṟṟut tarutal.

6. kālkaḷai piṉpuṟamāka vēkamāka utaittu muṉṉēṟum valimaiyai vaḷarttu viṭutal. (Leg kick)



7. taṇṇīraik kaikaḷāl kiḻittu, otukki muṉṉēṟum vallamaiyaik kaṟṟut tarutal. (Arm movement)

piṟaku, ovvoru nīccal muṟaiyaiyum, toṭarntu kaṟṟut taralām.

āṉāl, nīccal payiṟciyil muḻu nēramum, āciriyar arukē irukka vēṇṭum. avaratu kaṇkāṇippum utaviyum atikamāka irukka vēṇṭum.

āciriyar inta viṣayattil eccarikkaiyākavum, kavaṉamākavum iruppatu mika mika avaciyamākum.





﻿11. toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ

(TOURNAMENTS)



* * *





viḷaiyāṭṭu eṉpatu, taṉakkuriya tiṟamaiyai terintu koṇṭu, makiḻcci peṟuvataṟkāka ēṟpaṭṭatākum.

viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭi eṉpatu, taṉatu tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ maṟṟavarkaḷ tiṟamaiyai oppiṭṭup pārttuk koḷḷa ēṟpaṭṭatākum.

taṉatu tiṟamai maṟṟavarkaḷ tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ caricamamāka irukkiṟatā, allatu carintupōy kiṭakkiṟatā eṉpataip purintu koṇṭu, mēlum tiṟamaikaḷil tērcci koḷḷa, eḻucci peṟa, uṇarcci koḷḷa, pōṭṭikaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa.

appaṭippaṭṭa aṟivārnta nilaiyil, uyarnta taṉmaiyil amaintuḷḷa pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷai, eppaṭi, naṭatta vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku oru niyatiyum nērmaiyum irukka vēṇṭumallavā!

ellārkkum camavāyppu, camaantastu eṉṟa jaṉanāyakap paṇiyilē uruvākkappaṭṭirukkum, pōṭṭit toṭar pantayaṅkaḷ paṟṟi, iṅkē virivākak kāṇpōm.



toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭai nōkkam veṟṟi tōlviyai viḷaivittuk koṭuppatu tāṉ eṉṟālum, vaḻimuṟaikaḷum, vāyppu nilaikaḷum koñcam māṟiyē tāṉ amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa.

ellā cālaikaḷum rōm nakarai nōkkiyē celkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu oru mēṉāṭṭup paḻamoḻi.

ellā toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷum, āṭṭakkārarkaḷiṉ tiṟamaiyai matittu; āṭum yuktiyai vaḷarttu. pōṭṭi naṭattupavar, paṅku peṟupavar. naṭuvarākap paṇi yāṟṟukiṟavar, pārvaiyāḷarkaḷ eṉṟu ellōrukkumē ellām taruvatāka amaintiruppatāl tāṉ, palvēṟu vitamākap pirintu, pāṅkāṉa nōkkuṭaṉ, paṇpāṉa, amaippuṭaṉ paṇipurintu, pārāṭṭukkaḷaip peṟṟuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

iṉi, pantayaṅkaḷiṉ pala vakai pirivukaḷ paṟṟi aṟintu koḷvōm.



I oru vāyppu muṟai allatu nīkkumuṟaip pantayaṅkaḷ

(Knock out or Elimination Tournaments)

itilē irukkiṉṟa vevvēṟu pirivukaḷ.

a) orē oru vāyppu muṟai (Single elimination)



ā) āṟutal vāyppu muṟai (Consolation Type)



i) iraṭṭai vāyppu muṟai (Double Knock out)



ī) pākṉāl oyilṭu vāyppu muṟai (Bagnall wild elimination)





II toṭar vāyppup pantayaṅkaḷ allatu cuḻal muṟai pantayaṅkaḷ (League or Robinhood Tournaments)

a) oru vāypput toṭar (Single league)

ā) iraṭṭai vāypput toṭar (Double league)

III oruṅkiṇainta toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ (Combination Tournaments)

a) oru vāyppu muṟaiyuṭaṉ oru vāyppu muṟai (Knock out Cum Knock out)

ā) oru vāyppu muṟaiyuṭaṉ toṭar vāyppu muṟai (Knock out Cum League)

i) (pala) toṭar vāyppu muṟaiyuṭaṉ toṭar vāyppu muṟai (League Cum League)

ī) toṭar vāyppu muṟaiyuṭaṉ oru vāyppu muṟai (League Cum Knock out)

IV cavāl pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ (Challenge Tournaments)

a) ēṇi muṟai (Ladder)

ā) kōpura aṭukku muṟai (Pyramid)

ittaṉai pirivukaḷ etaṟkeṉṟa aiyam, uṅkaḷukku ēṟpaṭuvatu iyaṟkaiyē.

oru pōṭṭit toṭarai naṭattuvatu eṉṟāl, kīḻk kāṇum nikaḻccikaḷai aṉucarittut tāṉ ēṟpāṭu ceytiṭa vēṇṭum.

1. pōṭṭiyai naṭattukiṉṟa kālam (Season)

2. pōṭṭiyai naṭattakkūṭiya nēram (Time)

3. irukkiṉṟa āṭukaḷa vacati, utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ (Play ground & Equipment)

4. pōṭṭi naṭattukiṟa viḷaiyāṭṭiṉ taṉmai (Type of game)

5. āṭṭa atikārikaḷ eṇṇikkaiyiṉ aḷavu (Officials)

6. pōtiya poruḷātārattaip poṟuttu (Finance)

iṉi, ovvoru vakai toṭarpōṭṭip pantayattai naṭatta, pōṭṭi niral (Fixtures) tayārikkum muṟaiyiṉaiyum, ataṟkāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu mūlam aṟintu koḷvōm.



1. oru vāyppup pōṭṭi muṟai (Single Knock out or Single Elimination)

itai nīkkumuṟai pōṭṭi eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ.

inta oru vāyppup pōṭṭit toṭaril, paṅku peṟukiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷ, oru muṟai tōṟṟup pōṉālē, pōṭṭiyiliruntu iṭamiḻantupōkiṉṟaṉa. mīṇṭum āṭa vāyppē ataṟku kiṭaiyātu. kiṭaikkātu.

intap pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟukiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaikku ēṟpa, pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyum amaiyum.

utāraṇamāka, 12 kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟāl, naṭakkiṉṟa pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷ 11 eṉṟu amaiyum.

itaṟkāṉa cūttiram n-1 eṉpatu.

N eṉṟāl Number of Teams eṉpatu arttam.



atil oṉṟaik kaḻittāl, ettaṉai āṭṭam eṉṟu teriyum.

ākavē 12 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl, 12-1 = 11 eṉṟu terintu koḷḷalām.

pōṭṭi niral tayārikkum muṟai (Drawing Fixtures)

pōṭṭiyil kalantu koḷkiṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai- iraṭṭaippaṭaiyil, 2ṉ perukkattil iruntāl, ataṟkāṉa pōṭṭi niral tayārikkum muṟai vēṟu. atāvatu 2, 4, 6, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, eṉṟa kaṇakkāka irukkum pōtum iraṭṭaip perukkattiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil amaintirukkāmal, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 17liruntu 31, 33liruntu 63 eṉpatāka iruntāl, pōṭṭi niraliṉ tayāripput taṉmai vēṟāka irukkum.

iraṭṭaip perukka eṇṇikkaiyil uḷḷa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai 8 eṉṟu koḷvōm. ataṟkāṉa pōṭṭi niralai eppaṭi tayārippatu eṉṟu pārppōm.

1. cariyāṉa aḷavuḷḷa kākitattuṇṭukaḷ 8 eṭuttu, avaṟṟil 1 mutal 8 eṇkaḷai taṉittaṉiyē eḻuti, curuṭṭi vaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. piṟaku ovvoru kākitac curuḷaiyum kulukki eṭuttu, atiluḷḷa eṇṇaip paṭittu, ataṉpaṭi varicaiyāka eḻuta vēṇṭum. atāvatu, mēl taṭṭil uḷḷa (Top), aṭittaṭṭil uḷḷa eṇkaḷukkēṟṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai eḻuta vēṇṭum.

3. mutal cuṟṟu āṭṭattiṟkāṉa kuḻukkaḷai aṭaippukkuṟi mūlam iṇaittu, āṭakkūṭiya tēti, nēram, iṭam mutaliyavaṟṟaik kuṟikka vēṇṭum.

4. iraṇṭām cuṟṟu mutal iṟutic cuṟṟu varai, toṭarntu aṭaippuk kuṟiyil kāṭṭiṭa vēṇṭum.

8 kuḻukkaḷ eṉkiṟapōtu, mēl taṭṭil 4m, kīḻt taṭṭil 4m, eṉṟum amaiyum.

iṉi, pōṭṭi niral paṭṭiyalaip pārppōm.



1. 8 aṇikaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral





2. 11 aṇikaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral

pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai 2ṉ perukkattil illāmal iruntāl (8, 16, 32); ataṟ kēṟpa pōṭṭi niralait tayārikka vēṇṭum eṉpatai nām aṟivōm.

ataṟku utavuvatutāṉ ciṟappiṭam tarukiṉṟa vilakku muṟai (Bye)

ettaṉai kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟu aṟintu koṇṭa piṉ, anta eṇṇikkaiyai nām uṟuti ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

11 kuḻukkaḷ vantiruntāl 5m,

12 kuḻukkaḷ vantiruntāl 4m,

13 kuḻukkaḷ vantiruntāl 3m,

20 kuḻukkaḷ vantiruntāl 12m eṉṟa eṇṇikkaiyil ciṟappu vilakku (Bye) koṭukka vēṇṭum.

itu eppaṭi varukiṟatu? ēṉ koṭukka vēṇṭum? eṉṟu kēḷvikaḷ eḻuvatu uṇmai tāṉ.

nām iraṇṭiṉ perukkam eṉṟu mutalil kūṟiṉōm allavā! atu 2,4, 8, 16, 32,64 eṉṟu varukiṟatu allavā!

6 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl 8kku uṭpaṭṭatallavā! appoḻutu ciṟappu vilakku peṟuvatu 2 kuḻukkaḷ. 8-6 = 2 11 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl 16kku uṭpaṭṭatallavā! appoḻutu ciṟappu vilakku peṟuvatu 5 kuḻukkaḷ. 16–11 = 5

20 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl 32kku uṭpaṭṭatallavā! appoḻutu ciṟappu vilakku peṟuvatu 12. 32-20 = 12

ippaṭiyāka, nām kaḻittu, muṭivu ceytuviṭa vēṇṭum.

ciṟappu vilakku peṟukiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷ, mutal cuṟṟukku maṭṭumē viḷaiyāṭāta vāyppaip peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

oru pōṭṭi āṭṭattai āṭāmal iruppataṉālē, antak kuḻukkaḷukkup peritākap payaṉ ētum viḷaintuviṭap pōvatillai.

oru cuṟṟu āṭṭam āṭiya piṟaku, ōyvu kiṭaittālum nallatu. cuṟṟukku muṉṉē ōyvu kiṭaippatāl eṉṉa lāpam?

eṉavē, mutal cuṟṟu āṭṭattiṟku maṭṭum ciṟappu vilakkut tarukiṉṟa kuṟippai maṭṭum nīṅkaḷpurintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.

ciṟappu vilakku aḷikkiṉṟa muṟai

1. 5 kuḻukkaḷ ciṟappu vilakku peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟāl, anta 5 kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyum, taṉittaṉiyē oru tuṇṭuk kākitattil eḻuti, curuṭṭi vaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

3. patiṉōru kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟāl, anta 11 kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyum varicaiyāka eṇkaḷ tantu, paṭṭiyalil nirappa vēṇṭum.



3. piṟaku, 5 tuṇṭuc curuḷkaḷiliruntu, oṉṟu oṉṟāka, oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka eṭuttup pārttu, mutalil varukiṉṟa kuḻuviṉ peyarai, kīḻt taṭṭu paṭṭiyalil uḷḷa 11vatu iṭattil eḻuta vēṇṭum. mutal vilakku, kīḻppaṭṭiyalil kaṭaici. 2m vilakku mēl paṭṭiyalil mutal iṭam 1, 3vatu vilakku kīḻppaṭṭiyalil 10. nāṉkāvatu vilakku mēlpaṭṭiyalil 2. 5vatu vilakku kīḻil uḷḷa 9vatu iṭam. inta muṟaiyai aṭaippuk kuṟippil kāṇpittirukkiṟōm. aṟika.



11 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral



cuṟṟu 1﻿ cuṟṟu 2﻿ cuṟṟu 3﻿ cuṟṟu 4





pōṭṭi niralil kuḻukkaḷai tērnteṭukkum pōtu, cīṭṭuk kulukkip pōṭukiṟa muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟavum. itil mēlpāti, kīḻppāti eṉṟu nām aṭikkaṭi kuṟippiṭukiṟōm. itai eppaṭik kaṇṭupiṭippatu?

1. kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai iraṭṭaippaṭai eṇṇikkaiyil iruntāl eḷitu. ataṟkāṉa cūttiram . 10 aṇi eṉṟāl, mēlpāṭu yil 5m, kīḻppātiyil 5m varum.



2. kuḻukḷiṉ eṇṇikkai oṟṟaippaṭaiyil iruntāl, ataṟkāṉa cūttiram (n + 1)÷2.



mēlpātikkāṉatu itu.



um : 11 kuḻukkaḷukku : 11 + 1 = 12. = 6.



āka mēlpāti 6 kuḻukkaḷaik koṇṭatāka irukkum kīḻppātikku cūttiram : (n-1)÷2,



um : 11 kuḻukkaḷukku : 11-1 ÷ 10. = 5.





āka, mēlpāti 6. kīḻppāti 5 eṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.



ciṟappu vilakku (Bye) koṭukkiṟapōtu, kaṭaippiṭikka vēṇṭiya muṟai kīḻē tarappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

vilakkukku iraṭṭaippaṭai eṇṇāka aṇikaḷ iruntāl

mēlpātikkut taravēṇṭiya cūttiram nb÷2.



um : 6 ciṟappu vilakku eṉṟāl : = 3.



kīḻppātikkum atuvē cūttiram nb÷2.



um : 6 ciṟappu vilakku eṉṟāl : = 3.



vilakkukku oṟṟaippaṭai eṇṇāka iruntāl

mēlpātiyai aṟiya : (mb-1)÷2.



um : 5 vilakkuk kuḻukkaḷ : 5-1 = 4 ; = 2.



kīḻppātikkuriyatu : (mb + 1)-2,



um : 5 vilakkuk kuḻukkaḷ : 5 + 1 = 6 ; = 3.



ākavē, 11 kuḻukkaḷ paṅkupeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉkiṟapōtu, 6 ciṟappu vilakku eṇṇikkai varukiṟatu.

atil kīḻppātiyil 3m, mēlpātiyil 2m eṉkiṟa vikitattil, iṭampeṟṟu iruppataik kaṇṭu teḷika.

cila utāraṇaṅkaḷait tarukiṟōm

13 kuḻukkaḷ mottam eṉṟāl : 3 ciṟappu vilakku. ḻēppātiyil 2; mēlpātiyil 1.

19 kuḻukkaḷ mottam eṉṟāl 13 ciṟappu vilakku. kīḻppātiyil 7. mēlpātiyil 6.

27 kuḻukkaḷ mottam eṉṟāl 5 ciṟappu vilakku. iḻppātiyil 3. mēlpātiyil 2.

oru vāypput toṭar pōṭṭi pantayaṅkaḷil uḷḷa, kiṟaikaḷum kuṟaikaḷum.

niṟaikaḷ

1. pantayaṅkaḷaik kuṟitta nērattil, kuṟukiya kālattiṟkuḷ viraivāka naṭatti muṭikka muṭiyum.

2.ellām tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi naṭappatāl, atika poruṭcelavu varātu.

3.pantayattil oru vāyppu tāṉ uḷḷatu eṉpatāl, pōṭṭiyil atika ārvattōṭum, tīvira muyaṟciyōṭum kuḻukkaḷ paṅkupeṟum, tōṟkum kuḻu iṭam iḻantu pōkiṟatallavā!

kuṟaikaḷ

1. pōṭṭi oṉṟil etirpārāta vitamāka tōṟṟup pōkiṟa kuḻu, mīṇṭum taṉatu tiṟamaiyai veḷik kāṭṭa iyalāmal pōkiṟatu.

2. maṟṟa kuḻukkaḷ veṟṟi peṟṟu, ataṉuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭuvataṟkāka oru kuḻu, kāttirukka vēṇṭiyirukkiṟa kāla tāmatam nērkiṟatu.

3. cīṭṭuk kulukkip pōṭṭu, pōṭṭi niral tayārikkappaṭuvatāl, nalla tiṟamaiyuḷḷa aṇikaḷ oru pātiyil vantu, cantikkiṟapōtu, nalla kuḻu iṭamiḻantu pōka nēriṭukiṟatu.

cila camayaṅkaḷil tiṟaṉaṟṟa kuḻuvum tiṟamaiyāṉa kuḻuvum iṟutip pōṭṭiyil cantikka nēriṭuvatum uṇṭu; anta nilaiyai inta oru vāypput toṭar aṭikkaṭi uṇṭākki viṭukiṟatu.

intak kuṟaiyai nīkkattāṉ ciṟappiṭam tarum muṟai (Seeding Method) piṉpaṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.

oru pōṭṭikku 12 aṇikaḷ pōṭṭikku vantiruk kiṉṟaṉa. ciṟappu vilakku 4 aṇikaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟu nām aṟivōm.

anta iṭattil varuvataṟku, cīṭṭuk kulukkippōṭukiṟa muṟaiyil, muṉpu nām tērnteṭuttōm.

iṅku, ciṟappiṭam tarum muṟaiyil, cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlam aḷikkāmal, pōṭṭiyai naṭattupavarkaḷē aṇikaḷait tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.

eppaṭi tērnteṭuppatu?

ataṟku 2 vaḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

1. kaṭanta kālattil, ciṟappāka āṭiya aṇikaḷiṉ cātaṉaikaḷaik koṇṭum;

2. appaṭi teriyāviṭṭāl, nēril anta aṇikaḷ paṟṟi kēṭṭuk koṇṭum, tīrmāṉikkalām.

ivvāṟu tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷai maṭṭum (iṅkē 4 eṉṟu vaittuk koḷvōm) cīṭṭuk kulukkaliṉ mūlamāka allatu parinturai mūlamāka, ciṟappiṭam koṭuttiṭalām.

itil kaṭaipiṭikka vēṇṭiya mukkiya amcam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, paṅkupeṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷ 4m, varukiṟa tūram, peṟṟirukkiṟa tiṟamai, taram mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum kuṟippil koḷḷalām.

ceṉṉaiyil pōṭṭi naṭakkiṟatu. nākar kōvil, kōvai, tirucci, maturai eṉum iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu kuḻukkaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. anta aṇikaḷiṉ taramum tiṟamum naṉṟāka irukkiṟatu eṉpatāl, anta nāṉku aṇikkum ciṟappiṭam (Seeding) tarukiṉṟōm.

72 kuḻukkaḷukku ciṟappiṭam tantiṭum oru vāypput toṭar pōṭṭi niral





kuṟippu itu pōl kuḻukkaḷukku ciṟappiṭam tarukiṟa pōtu, arai iṟuti āṭṭattil, tiṟamaiyuḷḷa kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭi yiṭum vāyppu kiṭaikkiṟatu. ēṉeṉil, nalla kuḻukkaḷ 4m, mēṟpātiyil 2; kīḻppātiyil 2 eṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

2. ciṟappiṭam tarukiṟa kuḻuvai, parinturai mūlam pōṭalām. avaṟṟiṉ tiṟamaikaḷ cariyāka. irukkum paṭcattil, cīṭṭuk kulukkip pōṭṭut tērnteṭuppataṉ mūlam, cikkalai cariceytu viṭalām.

ivvāṟu ciṟappiṭam tantu, vilakkaḷikkum muṟaiyai (Seeding - byes) eppaṭi ceyvatu eṉpatai, iṉi nām kāṇpōm.

mottam 25 kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

avaṟṟil 8 aṇikaḷukku nām ciṟappiṭam taravēṇṭum.

8 aṇikaḷum orē pātiyil vantu viṭakkūṭātu.

mēl pāti, kīḻppāti eṉa pirittut tantālum, 4, 4 eṉṟu varukiṟa pōtu, oru pakutiyilēyum vantu viṭak kūṭātu.

ākavē, anta eṭṭu aṇikaḷum, kāl iṟutip pōṭṭikaḷil kalantu koḷvatu pōlac ceytāka vēṇṭum.

muḻu pōṭṭi niralaiyum nām ituvarai iraṇṭākap pirittu, mēlpāti, kīḻppāti eṉṟu peyar tantirukkiṟōm.



ippoḻutu mēlpātiyai iraṇṭākavum, kīḻp. pātiyai iraṇṭākavum pirittāka vēṇṭum. anta araippāti iraṇṭākap pirikiṟa pōtu, kāl pakuti (Quarter) eṉṟa peyaraip peṟukiṟatu.

eṉavē, iṅku nāṉku kālpakutikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟai nām 1, 2, 3, 4 eṉṟu peyariṭuvōm.

ovvoru kālpakutiyilum 2 aṇiyāka 4 kālpakutiyilum 8 aṇikaḷ iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

ivvāṟu iṭam peṟukiṟa ciṟappiṭam peṟṟa aṇikaḷ, kāl iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tāṉ, vantu pōṭṭiyiṭum.

appaṭiyeṉṟāl, araiyiṟutip pōṭṭiyil 4 aṇikaḷ, iṟutip pōṭṭiyil 2 aṇikaḷ eṉṟu iṭam peṟum.

itaṟkiṭaiyil ciṟappiṭaṅkaḷ peṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷ, mutal cuṟṟilēyē tōṟṟup pōṉālum pōkalām. atu vēṟu viṣayam.

nām ciṟappiṭam taruvatu eṉpatu tāṉ, iṅkē mukkiyamāṉatākum.

anta ciṟappiṭam tarukiṟa muṟaiyai, kīḻkkāṇum muṟaippaṭi taralām.

25 kuḻukkaḷukkuriya pōṭṭi niral





mukkiyak kuṟippukaḷ

1. 8 aṇikaḷ ciṟappiṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ taṉittaṉi tuṇṭu cīṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭu, cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlam eṭukkappaṭa vēṇṭum.



mēlē uḷḷa pōṭṭi niralaip pāruṅkaḷ.

atil ciṟappiṭam 1, ciṟappiṭam eṉṟu kuṟittirukkiṟōm.

atāvatu, cīṭṭuk kulukkalil mutalāvatāka varukiṉṟa aṇi kīḻpātiyil uḷḷa 4vatu kālpakutiyil kaṭaiciyilum: 2vatāka varukiṟa aṇi, mēlpātiyil uḷḷa 2vatu kāl pakutiyilum, ippaṭiyāka iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum.

kulukkalil mutal cīṭṭu-kīḻpātiyil-4vatu pakuti.



2vatāka varukiṟa cīṭṭu-mēlpāti-2vatu pakutiyil.



3vatāka varukiṟa cīṭṭu-kīḻpāti-3vatu pakuti.



4vatāka varukiṟa cīṭṭu-mēlpāti-1vatu pakuti.



mītiyai paṭam pārttup purintu koḷka.



25 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl, 7 ciṟappiṭam tāṉē uṇṭu. namakku. 8 aṇikaḷukku iṭam tara vēṇṭumē!

ataṟkāka, 8vatāka cīṭṭil varum aṇikku, mēl pātiyil uḷḷa 1vatu iṭattai aḷittiṭa vēṇṭum.

2. piṟaku, mītiyuḷḷa 17 iṭaṅkaḷukkāka, cīṭṭuk kulukkal pōṭṭu, avaṟṟai varicaiyākak kuṟittuk koṇṭē varavēṇṭum. atāvatu mēliruntu, kīḻākak kuṟittuk koṇṭuvara vēṇṭum.



3. ataṟkup piṟaku āṭavēṇṭiya kuḻukkaḷai iṇai iṇaiyāka (Pair) iṇaittu, aṭaippuk kuṟiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

tērnteṭuttu ciṟappiṭam tarum muṟai (Special Seeding)

oru āṭṭattil, tiṟamai peṟṟa āṭṭakkārarkaḷ allatu āṭum kuḻukkaḷ iruppatuṇṭu.

avaṟṟai avarai mutal cuṟṟiliruntu āṭaviṭāmal, nēraṭiyāka kāl iṟuti āṭṭattil kalantu koḷvatu pōla, ciṟappiṭam aḷikkum muṟaiyum irukkiṟatu.

im muṟai cariyāṉatu illaiyeṉṟālum, pōṭṭit toṭaril puttuṇarcciyai ēṟpaṭuttuvataṟkākavē kaiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟatu.

iṅkē nām 12 kuḻukkaḷa kalantu koḷḷum oru toṭar pōṭṭiyil, 4 aṇikaḷait tērntu ciṟappiṭam tarukiṟōm. antap pōṭṭip paṭṭiyalai eppaṭit tayārippatu eṉṟu pārppōm. 12 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral

mutal cuṟṟu kāl iṟuti arai iṟuti iṟuti āṭṭam





kuṟippu :

1. 4 kuḻukkaḷai mutalil tērnteṭuttuk koṉkiṟōm.



2. avaṟṟai cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlam poṟukki eṭukkiṟōm.



3. mutalāvatāka varukiṟa kuḻuvai — kīḻppāti kaṭaiciyil 12l kuṟikkiṟōm.



4. 2vatāka varuvatai mēlpāti kīḻil 6l kuṟikkiṟōm.



5. 3vatāka varuvatai kīḻppāti mēlil 7l. kuṟikkiṟōm.



6. 4vatāka varuvatai mēlpāti 1l kuṟikkiṟōm.

7. piṟaku kuḻukkaḷai iṇai iṇaiyāka cērttu viṭukiṟōm.

ippaṭiyāka, intap pōṭṭi niralait tayārikka vēṇṭum. itu pōl, ettaṉai kuḻukkaḷ vantālum, muṟaiyāka ciṟappiṭak kuḻukkaḷai tērnteṭuttu, ciṟappiṭa vilakkukaṉaik kaṇakkiṭṭu, niralai tayārikka vēṇṭum.

āṟutal vāyppu muṟai paktayaṅkaḷ

(Consolation Tournaments)



oru vāyppuḷḷa pōṭṭit toṭaril, tiṟamai. vāynta oru kuḻu ētō oru kāraṇattiṟkāka, acātāraṇa muṟaiyil tōṟṟuppōvatuṇṭu.

appaṭi ēṟpaṭukiṟa tōlviyāl, iṭamiḻantu pōkum. antat tiṟamaiyāṉa kuḻu, taṉatu muḻu tiṟamaiyaiyum. veḷippaṭuttik kāṭṭa muṭiyāmal pōy viṭuvatāl, appaṭippaṭṭa kuṟaiyaip pōkki, mīṇṭum āṭa vāyppaḷippataṉ mūlam, avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ muḻut. tiṟamaiyaiyum veḷippaṭuttikkāṭṭi, miñci irukkiṉṟa. veṟṟiyaiyum, paricukaḷaiyum, pārāṭṭukkaḷaiyum peṟa. vaḻi ēṟpaṭutti vāypput taruvatē, āṟutal vāypput toṭar pōṭṭi eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu.

itaṉai, iraṇṭu vakaiyākki, vāypput taruvārkaḷ. mutalil mutal vakaiyaip pārppōm. (First Type) 13 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa āṟutal vāyppuk toṭar pōṭṭi





mutal cuṟṟil tōṟṟa 2, 4, 5, 9, 11 ciṟappiṭam peṟṟa mutal cuṟṟil āṭāmal, 2m cuṟṟil āṭit tōṟṟa 13m, āṟutal pōṭṭiyil iṭam peṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉa

avaṟṟiṟku. nām pōṭṭi niral tayārikka vēṇṭum.

tōṟṟirukkum kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai 6.

ākavē, itil 2 ciṟappiṭa vilakku nām aḷikka vēṇṭum.



āṟutal vāyppu pōṭṭi niral





kuṟippum vilakkamum

1. inta āṟutal vāyppil, tōṟṟa kuḻu, iraṇṭāvatāka viḷaiyāṭukiṟa vāyppaip peṟukiṟatu.

2. tōṟṟak kuḻuviṉar taṅkaḷukkuḷḷē, mīṇṭum vāypput toṭar pōṭṭiyai āṭi, niṟaivu koḷkiṉṟaṉar.

3. mutal cuṟṟil tōṟṟa kuḻukkaḷaiyum, ciṟappiṭam peṟṟu 2vatu cuṟṟil āṭit tōṟṟa kuḻukkaḷaiyum, intap pōṭṭi niralil cērttuk koḷḷalām.

4. āṟutal vāyppup pōṭṭikkāṉa pōṭṭi niral tayārikkiṟa poḻutum, iraṭṭaip perukkam, atil vilakkam tarukiṟa vitimuṟaikaḷaiyum, iṅkē kaṭaipiṭittiṭa vēṇṭum.

5. atikamāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ naṭaipeṟuvatāl, pōṭṭit toṭarāṉatu puttuṇarcci ḷittiṭum perumaiyaiyum vāyppaiyum peṟukiṟatu.

4 ciṟappuvilakku kuḻu (Bye) = 4 — 1 = 3 pōṭṭikaḷ atika aḷavu : 8 ciṟappu vilakku peṟāta kuḻukkaḷ

ākavē 8 — 1 = 7 pōṭṭikaḷ

3. kalantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷ, naṭuvṉ eṇṇukkum kīḻāṉatāka iruntāl (less than the middle number)

naṭuvaṉ eṇṇuḷḷa amaippiṉpaṭiyē, āṟutal āṭṭaṅkaḷ āṭappaṭum.

naṭuvaṉ eṇṇukkum atikamāṉa amaippil, kuḻukkaḷ iruntāl 2ai kaḻikka vēṇṭum.

um : 10 kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

kuṟainta paṭca āṭṭam kaṇṭupiṭikka :10kku naṭueṇ 5 eṉṟu koḷḷavum.

ākavē, 5 — 2 = 3 āṟutal āṭṭaṅkaḷ kuṟainta paṭcam. atikapaṭca āṟatal āṭṭam evvaḷavu eṉpatai aṟiya (n - minus 2 minus minimum) eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭuka.

motta kuḻu eṇṇikkai 10.

atil 2ai kaḻittāl —

kuṟaintapaṭca āṭṭaṅkaḷai kaḻittāl 10 — 2 — 3 = 5

āka, 5 pōṭṭi āṟutal āṭṭaṅkaḷai naṭatta vēṇṭum.

4. kalantu koḷḷum kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai, naṭuvaṉ eṇṇukkum atikamāka iruntāl. (more than the middle number).

ataṟkāṉa cūttiram - naṭuvaṉ eṇ ettaṉaiyō anta eṇṇikkai tāṉ kuṟainta paṭca pōṭṭi āṭṭam.

atika aḷavu āṭṭam kaṇṭupiṭikka- (n- minus 2 and minus maximum)

utāraṇattiṟku pārppōm.

15 kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

kuṟainta paṭcam pōṭṭi āṭṭam : naṭuvaṉ eṇ 15kku 7.

ākavē 7 āṟutal āṭṭaṅkaḷ āṭalām. atikapaṭca āṭṭaṅkaḷ kāṇa : 15-2-7 = 6āṭṭaṅkaḷ

iṉi, āṟutal pōṭṭiyiṉ 2vatu vakaiyaip pārppōm.

inta iraṇṭāvatu vakai āṟutal pōṭṭiyil, pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟu vāyppiḻanta ellāk kuḻukkaḷumē, pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, ataṟkuriya veṟṟip perumaiyai aṭaiya vāyppu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.

ārampac cuṟṟukaḷil mōtiya kuḻukkaḷaiyē, mīṇṭum mōtaviṭāmal tavirttu, pōṭṭi niralait tayārikka vēṇṭum.

intap pōṭṭi niralai iraṇṭu muṟaiyāka nām tayārikkalām.

1. ciṟappu vilakku tarātapaṭi tayārittal.

2. ciṟappiṭam (vilakku) koṭuttu tayārittal.

ippaṭi tōṟṟavarkaḷait tērnteṭuttuk koṇṭu, avarkaḷukku iṭaiyē pōṭṭi niralai mīṇṭum tayārikka vēṇṭum.





intap pōṭṭiyil 9m kuḻu āṟutal veṟṟiyāḷa rākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.

inta pōṭṭi niral muṟai eppaṭi amaintatu eṉṟu iṅkē viḷakkamākak kāṇpōm.

1. eṭṭu kuḻu mutalil tōṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟukku iṭaiyē pōṭṭi naṭatti, 4 kuḻuvai tērnteṭukkiṟōm.



2. iraṇṭāvatu cuṟṟil tōṟṟuḷḷa 4 kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ, mutal cuṟṟil veṟṟi peṟṟa nāṉku kuḻuvaiyum pōṭṭi iṭa vaikkiṟōm. 3. inta 2vatu cuṟṟil veṟṟi peṟṟu varukiṟa 2 kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ, 3vatu cuṟṟil tōṟṟirunta 2 kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭa vaikkiṟōm.

4. itil jeyitta oru kuḻuvai iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa kuḻuvuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭa vaikkiṟōm.

iṉi, ciṟappiṭam koṭuttu (Byes) eppaṭi inta āṟutal pōṭṭi niralait tayārikka vēṇṭum eṉpataip pārppōm.

1. mutal cuṟṟil oru oru kuḻu tāṉ tōṟṟirukkiṟatu.

2. 2vatu cuṟṟil 4 kuḻukkaḷ tōṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.

3. 2vatu cuṟṟil tōṟṟataiviṭa, mutal cuṟṟil tōṟṟa kuḻu kuṟainta eṇṇikkaiyil iruppatāl, ataiyum intak kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ cērttuk koṇṭu, 3vatu cuṟṟil tōṟṟa kuḻu eṇṇikkaikku ēṟpa, kuṟaivāka varuvatu pōl, ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

4. maṟṟa cuṟṟukkaḷukku, muntaiya āṟutal niralaik kāṇka.

ippoḻutu, ciṟappiṭam tanta, 9 kuḻukkaḷukkuriya pōṭṭi niralaik kāṇpōm.

10 kuḻukkaḷ—ciṟappiṭam tantuḷḷa pōṭṭi niral





6 ciṟappiṭam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

āṟutal pōṭṭi niral





itil 9, veṟṟik kuḻuvāka aṟivikkappaṭukiṟatu.

inta 2vatu muṟaiyil uḷḷa ciṟappamcam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, tōṟṟa kuḻukkaḷai, ovvoru cuṟṟāka eṭuttu, pōṭṭiyiṭa vaittiruppatu tāṉ.

ovvoru cuṟṟilum pōṭṭiyiṭṭa kuḻukkaḷil veṉṟa kuḻu, aṭutta cuṟṟil veṟṟi peṟṟa kuḻuvuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, iṟutiyāka, veṟṟi nilaikku varuvatu tāṉ ciṟappamcamākum.

inta 2vatu vakaiyil uḷḷa āṟutal pōṭṭi niralil, 2vatu muṟaiyē ciṟantatāka, vallunarkaḷāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

muṟaiyāka veṟṟinilaiyai aṭainta kuḻu veṟṟi yāḷarākavum (Winner) , āṟutal pōṭṭiyil veṟṟiyai aṭainta kuḻuvai 2vatu veṟṟik kuḻuvākavum (Runner8 tp) aṅkīkarikkappaṭukiṟatu.

inta iraṇṭu muṟaikkum uḷḷa oṟṟumai-muṉṟaiyāka pōṭṭiyil veṟṟi peṟṟu vanta kuḻuvai mutaliṭam koṭuttu pārāṭṭuvatu tāṉ.

āṉāl, āṟutal pōṭṭiyil veṟṟi peṟṟa kuḻuvai 2vatu veṟṟik kuḻuvāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭālum, antak kuḻu ellāk kuḻukkaḷiṭamum viḷaiyāṭi vellāmal, āṟutal pōṭṭik kuḻukkaḷai maṭṭumē veṉṟuvaruvatāl, itu oru kuṟaiyākavē irukkiṟatu.

itil ettaṉai pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭakkum eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟiya cūttiram (2n-3).

3. iraṭṭai vāyppu muṟai (Double Knock Out)

iraṭṭai vāyppu muṟai toṭar pōṭṭip pantayattil, oru kuḻu, pōṭṭiyiliruntu nīkkappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟāl, atu iraṇṭu muṟai tōṟkaṭikkappaṭa vēṇṭum.

pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷ aṉaittum, iraṇṭu muṟai tōṟkiṟa varaiyil, intap pōṭṭit toṭar toṭarum.

intap pōṭṭiyāṉatu, oṟṟai vāyppu muṟai, āṟutal vāyppu muṟai pantayaṅkaḷai viṭa ciṟantatākum.

ituvum āṟutal pōṭṭit toṭar muṟaiyiṉ, iraṇṭāvatu vakaiyaic cērntatākum. ēṉeṉṟāl, muṟaiyākap peṟṟa veṟṟik kuḻuvum (RW), āṟutal pōṭṭi niralil veṟṟi peṟṟa kuḻuvum pōṭṭiyiṭṭa piṟaku tāṉ, uṇmaiyāṉa veṟṟik kuḻuvai terintu koḷḷa muṭiyum.

itaṟkāṉa cūttiram (2n—2) allatu (2n—1).

9 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa iraṭṭai vāyppu muṟai pōṭṭi niral





kuṟippu : 8m nampar kuḻu, 1m nampar kuḻuvukkup patilāka veṟṟi peṟṟiruntāl, iraṇṭu kuḻukkaḷum, mīṇṭum viḷaiyāṭiyē ākavēṇṭum. ēṉeṉṟāl, kuḻu 8m 1m, oru muṟaitāṉ tōṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉpatai aṟiyavum. 4. pakṉāl oylṭ nīkku muṟai pōṭṭit toṭar

(Bagnall wild Elimination Tournament)

uṇmaiyāṉa mutal 3 veṟṟik kuḻukkaḷaiyum intap pōṭaṭi muṟai naṉku terivu ceyyum vāyppiṉai nalkukiṟatu.

10 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral





eppaṭi mutal mūṉṟu iṭam vantatu eṉṟu iṅkē kāṇpōm. mutal nilai I : oru vāyppu muṟai pōṭṭiyiṉ mūlamāka 6 mutal iṭam peṟṟu viṭṭatu.

iraṇṭām nilai 1 : mutaliṭam peṟṟa (6) kuḻuviṭam iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa (4) kuḻuvaittavira, maṟṟa kuḻukkaḷ mīṇṭum pōṭṭiyiṭukiṟa vāyppaip peṟṟu. atil mutalāvatāka varukiṟa kuḻu (7), iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa (4) kuḻuvuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭa iraṇṭām nilai nirṇayikkappaṭukiṟatu.

mīṇṭum pārppōm. 6 mutalnilai peṟṟa kuḻu. 6 iṭam tōṟṟa kuḻu 4 mīṇṭum tōṟṟa kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭi āyiṭukiṟapōtu, 9m 7m mōtukiṟatu, atil 7 veṟṟi peṟukiṟatu.

veṟṟi peṟṟa 7m nampar kuḻuvum, muṉṉar iṟutim pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa 4m nampar kuḻuvum pōṭṭiyiṭa 4m tampar kuḻu jeyikkavē, atu iraṇṭām iṭattaip peṟukiṟatu.

mūṉṟām nilai III : iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟak kuḻuviṭam (4) tōṟṟuppōṉa kuḻukkaḷ (mēlpakutiyil uḷḷatu) taṅkaḷukkuḷḷē pōṭṭiyiṭṭu: atil veṟṟi peṟukiṟa kuḻu iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟak kuḻuviṭam mōta, atil vellukiṟa kuḻu 2m iṭattaip peṟukiṟatu.

um : iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa 4m nampar kuḻuviṭam 1m 5m tōṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. 4m 5m pōṭṭiyiṭa, atil veṟṟipeṟukiṟa kuḻuvāṉa 5m namparuṭaṉ (kīḻppātiyil) aṭutta pātiyil veṟṟi peṟṟuḷḷa 7m namparuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭa vēṇṭum. itil vellukiṟa kuḻu 3m iṭattaip peṟukiṟatu.

kuṟippu : intap pōṭṭiyil 4m nampar jeyippataṟkup patilāka, 7m nampar kuḻu jeyittuviṭṭāl, 7m nampar kuḻu 2m iṭattaip peṟukiṟatu. tōṟṟa 4m nampar kuḻu, muṉṉarē, iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟiruntālum 7m nampariṭam tōṟṟataṉ kāraṇamāka, mūṉṟām nilaiyaip peṟukiṟatu.



11. toṭar vāyppup pantayaṅkaḷ

(League or Round Robin Tournment)

intap pantaya muṟaiyai, 2 vakaiyākap pirikkalām.

1. oru toṭar vāyppumuṟai. (Single league)

2. iraṭṭait toṭar vāyppu muṟai Double league)

itaṉāl ēṟpaṭum mottam pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai ataṟkāṉa kuttiram n (n-1) + 2.

motta pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷ 28 ākum.

um: 8 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl 8 (8-1) = 8x 7 = 56

toṭar vāyppup pantayattil uḷḷa niṟaiyum kuṟaiyum

niṟaikaḷ

1. pōṭṭiyiṭum kuḻukkaḷiliruntu, uṇmaiyāṉa tiṟamaivāynta veṟṟik kuḻuvaik kaṇṭupiṭikka utavukiṟatu.

2. atikamāṉa eṇṇikkaiyil pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaṉ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.

3. kuḻukkaḷiṉ tiṟamaiyai varicaippaṭutta itu utavukiṟatu.

4. oru vāyppu muṟaiyil naṭappatu pōla, oru kuḻu āṭi jeyittu varukiṟa varaikkum, maṟṟa kuḻukkaḷ vīṇākak kāttuk koṇṭirukka vēṇṭiya avaciyamillai. āṭuvataṟkut tayārarka enta kuḻu irukkiṟatō, ataṉuṭaṉ āṭiṭa muṭiyum.

kuṟaikaḷ

1. atikamāṉa pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟuvatāl, atika nēram eṭuttuk koḷkiṟatu. vacatikaḷ ceytu koṭukka vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, poruṭ celavum atikamākiṟatu.

2. aṭikkaṭi tōṟkiṟa kuḻu, uṟcākam iḻantu, pōkiṟatu. pōṭṭiyil atika īṭupāṭu koṉvatum ataṉāl kuṟaikiṟatu.

3. paṭikkaṭṭu muṟai (Staircase method)

7 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral





intap paṭikkaṭṭu muṟaiyil uḷḷa kuṟaikaḷāvaṉa:-

1. kuḻukkaḷ ellām oṉṟaiyoṉṟu cantittu āṭa vēṇṭiya cuṟṟukkaḷai, nām itil kaṇṭaṟivatillai.

﻿ 2. vaṭṭamuṟai, aṭṭavaṇaimuṟai ivaikaḷaip pōla, tayārittuḷḷa paṭikkaṭṭup pōṭṭi niral muṟaiyil, ententa nāṭkaḷil ententak kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭiyiṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatai eḷitil kuṟittuk kāṭṭavum iyalātu.

3. pantayattil kalantu koḷkiṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai oṟṟaippaṭaiyāka iruntāl, ententa cuṟṟil (Round) ententak kuḻuviṟku ciṟappiṭam aḷittu vilakkaḷikka (Bye) vēṇṭum, eṉpataiyum aṟintu koḷḷa iyalātu.

toṭar vāyppup pantayattil, veṟṟik kuḻuvait tīrmāṉikkum muṟai (To decide the winners)

1 pōṭṭi āṭṭattil veṟṟi peṟṟāl -

1 pōṭṭi āṭṭattil tōṟṟu viṭṭāl -

1 veṟṟi eṇ

kuḻuvum camanilai (Tie) aṭaintāl -

1 veṟṟi eṇ





pōṭṭit toṭaril, atikamāṉa veṟṟi eṇkaḷaip peṟukiṟa kuḻuvē, veṟṟi peṟṟatu eṉṟu aṟivikka vēṇṭum.

veṟṟi eṇkaḷ vikitattil, iraṇṭō allatu ataṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷ camanilai peṟṟiruntāl, antat taṭaiyait tīrttu, veṟṟikkuḻu etuveṉṟu tīrmāṉikka, kīḻkkāṇum vitimuṟaikaḷaip payaṉ paṭuttalām.

camakilaiyai camāḷittu veṟṟiyait tīrmāṉikkum vitikaḷ

1. camanilai peṟṟ 2 kuḻukkaḷil, mutal pōṭṭiyiṉ pōtu oṉṟai mutalil veṉṟuḷḷa kuḻuvaiyē veṟṟik kuḻuveṉṟu aṟivikkalām.

2. appoḻutum veṟṟi tōlviyai aṟiṉ muṭiyāta aḷavil cikkal iruntāl, camanilaiyil irukkum kuḻukkaḷil, entak kuḻu, atikamāṉa kuḻukkaḷai veṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉpataik, kaṇṭaṟintu, mutaliṭam koṭukkalām.

3. mīṇṭum tīrkka muṭiyātapaṭi cikkal iruntāl. entak kuḻu antap pōṭṭi muḻuvatilum atika veṟṟi eṇkaḷ peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa (Points), allatu atika kōlkaḷ (Goal) pōṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa eṉpataik kaṇṭaṟintu, muṭivai eṭukkalām.

mēlum purintu koḷvataṟkāka, tantiṭum cāṉṟiṉaim pāruṅkaḷ. AB eṉṟa 2 kuḻukkaḷ, hākkiyil camanilaiyil uḷḷaṉa. ataṟkāṉa muṭiviṉai aṟiya utavum paṭṭiyal itu.





﻿174

inta eṇṇikkai aḷavilum iru kuḻukkaḷum camanilaiyil iruntāl,

1. camanilaiyil uḷḷa kuḻukkaḷai, mīṇṭum orumuṟai pōṭṭiyiṭac ceytu, veṟṟi tōlviyai nirṇayikkalām.



1. atilum kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāmal allatu nēram illāmaṟpōṉāl, allatu muṭivaṟiya cātakamāṉa cūḻnilai amaiyāmal iruntāl, nāṇayam cuṇṭi, atil muṭivu kāṇalām. itai evarum virumpamāṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟālum, maṟṟavarkaḷ ēṟṟukkoṇṭāl maṭṭumē itaip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum. maṟṟa nilaiyil uḷḷa (1, 2 tavira kuḻukkaḷukku, atē iṭaṅkaḷai aḷittu, muṭivu kūṟi viṭalām.



III oruṅkiṇainta toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ (Combination Tournaments)

atikamāṉa eṇṇikkaiyil, kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṟapōtu, intap pantaya muṟaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.

varukiṟa kuḻukkaḷai pala pirivukaḷāka (Group) allatu pala tokutikaḷākap (Zones) pirittuviṭṭu, ovvoru pirivaiyum taṉittaṉiyē āṭaviṭṭu, avaṟṟil varukiṟa veṟṟik kuḻukkaḷai maṭṭum, oru vāyppu muṟai allatu pala vāypput toṭar muṟai mūlamāka āṭaviṭṭu, muṭivu kāṇalām.

tamiḻakam muḻuvatum kalantu koḷkiṟa pōṭṭi eṉṟu vaittuk koḷvōm. ellā kuḻukkaḷaiyum koṇṭu vantu, ōriṭattil pōṭṭi naṭattuvatu eṉpatu mikavum ciramamāṉa kāriyam.

ākavē, tamiḻakattai 4 tokutiyākap pirittu (A,B,C,D Zones) ovvoru tokutiyaiyum āṅkāṅkē āṭaviṭukiṟa muṟai.

tamiḻtāṭṭup paḷḷikaḷukkiṭaiyē naṭakkum kuṭiyaracup pōṭṭikaḷ, inta muṟaiyil tāṉ naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

﻿ 4. cavāl pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ (Challange Tournaments)

cavāl pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ oṟṟaiyar pantayam, allatu iraṭṭaiyar pantayam uḷḷa viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷukku maṭṭumē poruntum. (Singles and Doubles). pūppantāṭṭam, ṭeṉṉis, mēcaip pantāṭṭam, ṭeṉṉi kāṭṭ pōṉṟa āṭṭaṅkaḷai itaṟkuc cāṉṟākak kūṟalām.

kirikkeṭ, kaippantāṭṭam pōṉṟa mutaṉmai viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ pōṉṟa kuḻu viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷukku, itu tēvaippaṭātatu.

intap pōṭṭiyil kalantu koḷkiṟa aṉaivarum, oruvarukkoruvar taṉit taṉiyē pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, veṟṟi peṟṟu vara vēṇṭum.

inta muṟaiyil, taṉippaṭṭa ciṟanta vīrarkaḷai, kavaṉattuṭaṉ tērnteṭuttu viṭa muṭikiṟatu.

itaṟkāṉa pōṭṭi niralai. 2 vakaiyākap tayārikkalām.

1. ēṇi muṟai.

2. kōpura muṟai.

1. ēṇi muṟai (Ladder Method)

itai oraṭukku muṟai eṉṟu kūṭa kūṟalām.

ciṟanta āṭṭakkārarkaḷuṭaṉ, tiṟamaiyaṟṟa āṭṭakkārarkaḷ mōtuvatu eṉpatu itil illai. ellōrum ellāruṭaṉum pōṭṭiyiṭṭē āka vēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉ, inta muṟaiyiṉ ciṟappamcamākum.

cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlamākavō, terintu vaittirukkum taṉ muṉaippu mūlamākavō, pōṭṭi niralait tayārikkalām.

ataṟkāṉa, cila viti muṟaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟaiyum kāṇpōm.

1. intap pōṭṭit toṭar eppoḻutu toṭaṅki, eppoḻutu muṭiyum eṉpataik kaṭṭāyam kuṟip piṭṭāka vēṇṭum. (1 mātam allatu 8 mātam. eṉpatāka)





2. oru āṭṭakkārar, taṉakku mēltaṭṭil. uḷḷa oru āṭṭakkāraruṭaṉ tāṉ, pōṭṭiyiṭa vēṇṭum. allatu mēl varicaiyil uḷḷa 2. allatu 3 āṭṭakkārarkaḷaiyum. pōṭṭiyiṭa aḻaikkalām.

3. pōṭṭi niraliṉ paṭiyē tāṉ āṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟa niyatiyaik kaṭaipiṭikkcu ceyyalām.





4. pōṭṭikku aḻaittāl, ōriru nāṭkaḷukkuḷ antap pōṭṭi āṭṭattai, āṭi muṭittu viṭa vēṇṭum.





5. cavāliṭṭut tōṟṟup pōṉavar, ēṇi pōṭṭi niravil, muṉ irunta iṭattai, māṟṟik koṇṭāka vēṇṭum. illaiyeṉṟāl, avarkaḷ takutikkēṟpa, varicaiyākak koṭukkappaṭṭa iṭattilēyē iruntu āṭa aṉumatikkalām.



6. iruvar cavāliṭṭu āṭi muṭittu, veṟṟi tōlvi aṟinta piṟaku, mīṇṭum anta iruvarum pōṭṭiyiṭṭu āṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu avaciyam illai.

um : 3m 4m āṭiya piṟaku, 4 tōṟṟu viṭṭāl, mīṇṭum 3m 4m āṭa vēṇṭiyatillai. 4m eṇ, maṟṟa 2, 1 pōṉṟa namparkaḷuṭaṉ āṭalām.

7. pōṭṭiyiṭuvatait tavirppataṟkāka, cākku pōkkukaḷ kūṟiṉāl, atu ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭa māṭṭātu. iyaṟkaik kālanilai mōcamāka iruntāl maṭṭumē, āṭṭam māṟṟi vaikkappaṭalām.

appaṭi āṭavarātavar allatu maṟuppavar, tamatu takuti iṭattai viṭṭu māṟṟappaṭak kūṭiya nilaiyai aṭaikiṟār.

8. kuṟippiṭṭa kāla avakācattiṟkuḷḷē, ēṇip pōṭṭi niralil, mutal iṭattai vakittuk koṇṭirup pavarē, veṟṟiyāḷar eṉṟu aṟivikkappaṭuvār.

4–2. kōpura muṟai (Pyramid method)

ēṇi muṟaiyil caṟṟu māṟupaṭṭa vaṭivamaippuḷḷatu. itu.

ēṇi muṟaikkāṉa viti muṟaikaḷ. aṉaittum itaṟkup poruntum. cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlam allatu āṭṭakkārarkaḷait terinteṭuttup pōṭuvataṉ mūlamākap pōṭṭi niralait tayār ceyyalām.

taṉakkuriya pakutiyil iruntu (Rank) aṭuttu mēl varicaiyil uḷḷavarkaḷai maṭṭumē cavāl iṭṭu aḻaikkalām. atuvum taṉatu pakutiyil uḷḷavarkaḷuṭaṉ veṟṟi peṟṟiruntāl maṭṭumē atu iyalum.

kōpura pōṭṭi niral





12. kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷ (Group Competitions)

uṭaṟkalvip pāṭa vakuppukaḷil, palvēṟu vitamāṉa, uṭal iyakkac ceyalkaḷ, aṭippaṭait tiṟamaikaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkam tantu kaṟpikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.

ārvamuṭaṉ avaṟṟaik kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟa māṇavarkaḷ payiṟcikaṇait tiṟampaṭac ceytu, tiṟamaikaḷai vaḷarttuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.

vaḷarttuk koṇṭa tiṟamaikaḷai, maṟṟavarkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭṭut terintu koḷḷavum, mēlum mēlum tiṟamaikaṉai vaḷarttuk koḷḷavum kūṭiya vāyppukaḷai, kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷ koṭukkiṉṟaṉa.

ataṟkuriya vāyppukḷāka amaivaṉa-vakuppukaḷuk kiṭaiyē pōṭṭikaḷ. māṇavar pirivukaḷukkiṭaiyē pōṭṭikaḷ ākum.

kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷil kiṭaikkum naṉmaikaḷ

1. kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷ, māṇavarkaḷiṭaiyē ārvattaiyum, uṟcākattaiyum ūṭṭi vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa.

2. paṅku peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu māṇavarkaḷiṉ viruppam mēlum virivaṭaikiṟatu. vēkam koḷkiṟatu.

3. itaṉāl viḷaiyāṭṭut tiṟamaikaḷiṉ vaḷarcci mēlōṅkukiṟatu.

4. ovvoru māṇavarum taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil paṅku peṟuvatil ēṟpaṭukiṟa makiḻcciyaiyum, taṉatu kuḻuvukkut taṉ paṅkiṉai āṟṟukiṟōm eṉṟa tiruptiyaiyum peṟukiṉṟaṉar.

5. pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟuvatāl, kuṟippiṭṭa anta viḷaiyāṭṭil uḷḷa pala vitimuṟaikaḷai, teḷivākat terintu koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu.

6. viḷaiyāṭṭu pōṭṭikaḷiṉ mūlamāka, talaimai ēṟṟu naṭattal; talaimaikkup paṇital, ūkka muṭaṉ iṟutivarai pōṭṭiyiṭutal, veṟṟi tōlviyāl pātikkappaṭāta maṉam peṟutal ākiya paṇpāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷ meṉmēlum vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

ippaṭippaṭṭa kuḻu viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷukkup porutta māṉa viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷai cūḻnilai pārttu, tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.

iṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ, muṉṉōṭi viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ, cākacac ceyalkaḷ. taṉittiṟaṉ pōṭṭikaḷ, taṉinaṭaip vayiṟcikaḷ, oḻuṅku muṟai uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ, kōpurap payiṟcikaḷ, jimṉālṭik payiṟcikaḷ ellām ciṟappāṉa mūḻup pōṭṭikaḷākum,

kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷai kaṭattum muṟaikaḷ 1. 10 allatu 15 māṇavarkaḷ oru kuḻuvil iruppatu pōla, pala kuḻukkaḷaip pirittu, ovvoru kuḻuviṟkum oru kuḻut talaivaṉai niyamikkavēṇṭum.

2. kuḻukkaḷaip pirikkiṟapōtu, tiṟamaiyil, uṭal valimaiyil camamāka māṇavarkaḷ iruppatu pōla, pirikka vēṇṭum.

3. ovvoru kuḻuvaiyum aḻaippatu pōla, ētāvatu oru peyar taralām. (pūkkaḷ, mirukaṅkaḷ paṟavaikaḷ, niṟaṅkaḷ ētāvatu oṉṟil)

4. muṉṉatākac cuṟṟukkoṭutta viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷil ētāvatu oṉṟaip pōṭṭi viḷaiyāṭṭākat tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.

5. kuṟippiṭṭa nērattiṟkuḷ, muṭivu peṟuvatāka, antap pōṭṭi amaiya vēṇṭum.

6. kuḻukkaḷ peṟukiṟa veṟṟi nilaikku ēṟpa, veṟṟi eṇkaḷai aḷikka vēṇṭul. mutal iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku nilaikaḷukkāka 5, 3, 2, 1 eṉṟu veṟṟi eṇkaḷ taralām. 7. 200 catavikitam varukai tarukiṟa kuḻukkaḷukku, ciṟappu veṟṟi eṇkaḷ koṭukkavum ceyyalām.

8. varuṭat toṭakkattiliruntē, pōṭṭikaḷait. toṭaṅki, naṭattik koṇṭē vantu, varuṭak kaṭaiciyil paricukaḷ taralām.

itaṟkāṉa veṟṟi eṇ aṭṭavaṇaiyai piṉ pakkattil kāṇpōm.
