﻿13. uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ

(INTRAMURAL COMPETITÍONS)

Intramural eṉṟa āṅkilac collukku with in the walls atāvatu nāṉku cuvarkaḷukkuḷḷē epuaiṟa poruḷākum.

oru niṟuvaṉam allatu paḷḷi, kallūrikkuḷṉē taṭiai peṟukiṉṟa pōṭṭikaḷai uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu nām aḻaikkalām.

kōkkaṅkaḷ

1. kuṟippiṭṭa oru viḷaiyāṭṭu ceyalkaḷukkāṉa tiṟamaikaḷai vaḷarttu, māṇavarkaḷait tiṟamaiyākavumkaḷākka, uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa. 2. ellā māṇavarkaḷukkum cama vāyppu eṉkiṟa, muṟaiyil, (paḷḷi) māṇavarkaḷ aṉaivarum, atika eṇṇikkaiyil pōṭṭiyil kalantu koṇṭu, taṅkaḷ tiṟamaikaḷai veḷippaṭuttum vāyppiṉaip peṟukiṉṟaṉar.

3. talaimaiyēṟṟup piṟarai vaḻi naṭattavum, talaimai vakippavarkaḷiṉ vaḻi naṭantu piṉpaṟṟic cella vum kūṭiya pērāṇmai kuṇaṅkaḷai vaḷarttuviṭa.pōṭṭi kaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa.

4. makiḻcci, kēḷikkai, cantōṣam, tiruptiyil māṇavarkaḷ tiḷaikkum vāyppukaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. payiṟci niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil payilum māṇavarkaḷukku, pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattukiṉṟa aṉupavaṅkaḷ taruvatu, pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattuvatu, pōṭṭikaḷil atikāri نجینه (taḷākap paṇiyāṟṟuvatu, pōṉṟa kāriyaṅkaḷil nalla aṉupavaṅkaḷait tantu, āṟṟalai vaḷarttuttaravum uḷḷa kap pōṭṭikaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa.

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷai kaṭattum muṟaikaḷ

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattuvataṟku muṉpāka, ḻēkkāṇum kuṟippukaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu, kaṭaipiṭippatutallatu. 1. niṟuvaṉattiṉ taṉmai (māṇavarkaḷ viṭutiyil taṅkiyiruntu paṭikkum niṟuvaṉamā, vīṭṭiliruntu varupavarkaḷā eṉpataṟkēṟpa).

2. niṟuvaṉam irukkum iṭattiṉ taṭpa veppa amaippu.

3. irukkiṉṟa vacatikaḷ (āṭukaḷaṅkaḷ utavip poruṭkaḷ, utaviyāḷarkaḷ mutaliyaṉa).

4. poruḷātāra vacati.

5. pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattak kiṭaikkum nēram

6. māṇavarkaḷ peritum virumpip paṅkēṟkum viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ.

7. caka āciriyarkaḷiṭamiruntu kiṭaikkiṉṟa utavi

pōṭṭikaḷukkākap kuḻu pirittal

1. māṇavarkaḷai , avaravarkaḷ tiṟamaikkum cāmarttiyattiṟkum ēṟpa, pala pirivukaḷākap pirittu pōṭṭiyiṭac ceyyalām.

2. atikat tiṟamaiyuḷḷavarkaḷ tiṟamaiyuḷḷavarkaḷ, tiṟamai kuṟaintavarkaḷ eṉṟu mūṉṟu pirivukaḷākki, kuḻuk kaḷaip pirikkalām.

3. viṭutiyil taṅkip paṭippavarkaḷ atikam iruntu, viṭutikaḷ oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭu iruntāl, viṭutikaḷuk kiṭaiyē pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu naṭattalām.

4. viṭutikaḷ, vīṭṭiliruntu varupavarkaḷ, ippaṭiyum pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattalām. viṭutikaḷ pala iruppatāl, antanta viṭuti oru kuḻuvākum. vīṭṭiliruntu varupavarkaḷai, antanta ēriyā pārttup pirittu, pala kuḻukkaḷākkiyum pōṭṭiyiṭa vaikkalām.

5. viṭutikaḷ illāta paḷḷikaḷ, niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil, vakuppu vāriyākap pirikkalām : ciṟuvar, iḷaiyōr. mūttavar eṉṟum pākupaṭuttalām.

6 . kallūriyāka iruntāl, tuṟai vāriyākap (Department) pirittu, pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattalām.

ippaṭippaṭṭa pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattiṭa, ataiyum veṟṟikaramāka naṭattiṭa, ataṟkeṉṟu nirvākak kuḻu oṉṟai amaittiṭa vēṇṭum. antak kamiṭṭiyil iṭam peṟupavarkaḷ paṟṟi, iṉi aṟintu koḷvōm.

uḷḷakap pōṭṭi naṭattum kuḻu (Intramural Committee)



1. uḷḷakap pōṭṭik kuḻu iyakkunar (Intramural Director)



mūtta uṭaṟkalvi āciriyar oruvarē iyakkunarākap poṟuppu ēṟpār. maṟṟa uṭaṟkalvi āciriyarkaḷil oruvar, tuṇai iyakkunarākap poṟuppēṟṟu utavuvār. maṟṟa vakuppu āciriyarkaḷum pōṭṭi naṭaipeṟukiṟapōtu, utavi ceyvārkaḷ.



2. kuḻut talaivarkaḷum, kuḻut tuṇaittalaivarkaḷum

ovvoru kuḻuviṉ talaivarum, utavit talaivarum, pōṭṭi naṭattum kuḻuvil, uṟuppiṉarāki viṭukiṟārkaḷ ivarkaḷil oruvar ceyalarākavum, tuṇaic ceyalarākavum tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.

intap pōṭṭik kuḻutāṉ, pōṭṭi naṭattuvataṟkuriya vitimuṟaikaḷai uruvākkit tarum.

ceyalar poṟuppēṟṟuḷḷavar, pōṭṭikkuḻu naṭattukiṉṟa kūṭṭam, atil niṟaivēṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa nīrmāṉaṅkaḷ pōṭṭikaḷ paṟṟiya pōṭṭi niral, pōṭṭikaḷ , veṟṟi eṇ kuṟippēṭukaḷ, pōṉṟavaṟṟaip pativu ceytu, pattirappaṭutti vaittiṭal vēṇṭum.

etirppu maṉu ētāvatu vantāl, atṉait tīrkkum poṟuppu, pōṭṭik kuḻuvukkuriyatākum.

3. māṇavarkaḷukkup piṭitta poruttamāṉa viṉai yāṭṭukkaḷait tērnteṭuttal

mutaṉmai viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ, oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷ, kuṭṭikkaraṇamaṭittal, kōpurap payiṟcikaḷ, aṇi naṭai muṟaikaḷ, taṟkāppuk kalaikaḷ, tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷ ellām pōṭṭi naṭattuvataṟku poruttici" ceyalkaḷākum.

ivaṟṟil ētāvatu oṉṟait tērnteṭuttu, ataṉai pōṭṭip paṭṭiyalil cērttu, kamiṭṭiyiṭam aṉumatiyum peṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.

4. kāla nēra avakācam

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ āṇṭu muḻuvatu? naṭattappaṭa lām. kāl āṇṭu, araiyāṇṭut tērvu naṭattappaṭum kālattiṟkuḷḷē tāṉ (First and Second Term) atikamāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattappaṭa vēṇṭiyirukkum.

muṉṟāvatu paruvam (Term) muḻut tērvukkuriya kāla māṉatāl, appoḻutu pōṭṭikaḷ kuṟaikkappaṭṭu, paṭippukku mukkiyattuvam aḷikkiṉṟa avaciyattiṟku āḷāka vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu.

paḷḷi muṭinta piṟaku uḷḷa nēram. viṭumuṟai nāṭ kaṉil pōṭṭi.kaḷ naṭattuvatu poruttamāka irukkum. pantayaṅkaḷai oru vāyppu muṟai allatu toṭar vāyppu muṟaiyil naṭattalām.

5. veṟṟi eṇ vaḻaṅkutal

veṟṟi peṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ varicaikku ēṟpa, veṟṟi eṇkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭal vēṇṭum. mutal kuḻuvukku 5 , iraṇṭāvatu kuḻuvukku 3, mūṉṟāvatu kuḻuvukku ,ē nāṉkām kuḻuvukku 1 eṉpatāka pōṭṭi muṭintavuṭaṉ vaḻaṅki, atai veṟṟi eṇ aṭṭavaṇaiyil patintu viṭa vēṇṭum.

āṇṭu iṟutiyil, aṉaittup pōṭṭikaḷum muṭinta piṟaku, atikamāṉa veṟṟi eṇkaḷaip peṟṟirukkiṟa kuḻuvē, uḷḷakap pōṭṭi veṟṟik kuḻu (Intramural Champion) eṉṟu muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭum.

6. vekumatiyum pārāṭṭum

pōṭṭikaḷil veṟṟi peṟukiṟavarkaḷukku, niṉaivil iruppatupōl, nalla paricukaḷai vaḻaṅkalām. ovvoru viḷaiyāṭṭukkum oru veṟṟik kēṭayam allatu veṟṟik kōppaiyai vaḻaṅkalām. cuḻal kōppaikaḷum taralām.

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ pukaḻp palakai (Honour Board) eṉṟu oṉṟai amaittu, atil veṟṟik kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaip poṟittu vaikkalām.

veṟṟik kuḻuvaip pukaippaṭam eṭuttum, pukaḻp palakaiyil oṭṭi, veṟṟiyāḷarkaḷaip perumaippaṭuttalām.

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ paṟṟi cila kuṟippukaḷ

1. ciṟuvar, iḷaiyōr, mūttōr eṉṟu māṇavarkaḷaip pirittu, antantap pirivukkuḷ pōṭṭikaḷ naṭatti, ellā māṇavarkaḷaiyum pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟac ceyya vēṇṭum.

2. tiṟamaiyāṉavarkaḷāka kuḻukkaḷil iṭampeṟac ceytu, antanta āṟṟal mikka kuḻu, appaṭippaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭi iṭukiṉṟa cūḻnilaiyai amaittut tarutal vēṇṭum.

3. paḷḷikaḷ cārpāka viḷaiyāṭukiṟa tiṟamaimikka āṭṭakkārarkaḷ, uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟāmal ceyya vēṇṭum.

4. pala pōṭṭi ril oru cila māṇavarkaḷē paṅku peṟuvatait tavirttu ōriraṇṭu pōṭṭikaḷil maṭṭumē paṅku peṟalām ṉṟa vitiyai ēṟpaṭuttik kaṭṭuppaṭuttuvatu nallatu.

5. paḷḷikaḷil irukkiṉṟa iṭavacati, utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ, poruḷātāra vacati pōṉṟavaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttik koāvatu pōla, pōṭṭikaḷai naṭatta vēṇṭum.

6. muḻu aḷavil paṅku peṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷukku, atikamāka veṟṟi eṇkaḷai vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum.

7. māṟṟāṭṭakkārarkaḷai veḷiyē niṟuttiviṭṭu, nirantara āṭṭakkārarkaḷē āṭṭam muḻuvataiyum āṭi viṭuvārkaḷ. māṟṟāṭṭakkārarkaḷ kuṟaintatu 10 nimiṭam āṭavēṇṭum. (kāl pantāṭṭim) allatu 4 veṟṟi eṇkaḷ eṭukkum (kaip pantāṭṭam) varaiyilāvatu āṭukaḷattiṟkuḷ iṭam peṟṟu āṭavēṇṭum eṉkiṟa viṉai, kaṭṭāyappaṭutti, naṭaimuṟaippaṭutta vēṇṭum.

8. taṉippaṭṭa veṟṟiyāḷarkaḷ, allatu veṟṟik kuḻuviṟkum pārāṭṭum aḷittu keḷaravikka vēṇṭum.

ittaṉaik karuttukkaḷum, māṇavarkaḷ mikutiyāṉa eṇṇikkaiyil, uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟa ūkkuvikkum. uṟcākappaṭuttum. pōṭṭikaḷai veṟṟi mayamākkum.





﻿14. puṟa veḷip pōṭṭikaḷ





(EXTRAMURAL COMPETITIONS)





* * *



paḷḷikaḷukkiṭaiyē allatu (kalvi maṟṟum toḻil, niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkiṭaiyē naṭaipeṟukiṉṟa viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷai puṟa veḷip pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar.

pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟupavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ tiṟamai kaḷai maṟṟavarkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, veḷippaṭuttik kāṭṭi, taṅkaḷuṭaiya niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkup peyarum pukaḻum īṭṭit tarukiṉṟa vāyppukaḷai, puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

niṟaikaḷ 1. puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟupavarkaḷiṉ tiṟamaiyum valimaiyum tērcci peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

2. tāṅkaḷ cārntirukkum niṟuvaṉattiṉmēl taṉiyāta paṟṟum paktiyum perukum nilai, eḻucciyaṭaikiṉṟaṉa.

3. kuḻuttalaivar mēl mariyātai, avar collukkuppaṇital, paṇpāṉa viḷaiyāṭṭukkuṇaṅkaḷ vaḷartal, pōṉṟa ciṟanta paṇpāṭukaḷ vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

4. veḷiyāruṭaṉ paḻakum vāyppu ēṟpaṭuvatāl, putiya putiya naṭpum, uṟavum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.

5. pōṭṭikkākap pala iṭaṅkaḷukkum. pōvatāl, pala putiya iṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya aṟivum teḷivum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

6. pōṭṭikaḷiṉ mūlam, makiḻcci, tirupti, uṟcākam ellām niṟaiyavē kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.

kuṟaikaḷ 1. pōṭṭi eṉṟu vantu viṭṭālē, veṟṟipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa veṟiyum vēkamum vantu viṭukiṟatu. ataṉāl eppaṭiyāvatu veṟṟipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkāka, kuṟukku muṟaikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi, tavaṟiḻaittāvatu peṟṟāka vēṇṭum eṉṟu celkiṉṟa maṉappāṅku vaḷara vāyppirukkiṟatu.

2. cukātāramaṟṟa pōṭṭiyum, poṟāmai uṇarcciyum ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟatu.

3. atika nēram vīṇ ; atika paṇac celavu ; atika uṭal cakti iḻappu eṉṟum ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu.

4. māṇavarkaḷukkut tēvaiyaṟṟa maṉap pataṭṭam, maṉac cōrvu, maṉakkaḷaippu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.

5. cilar, taṅkaḷ tōlviyait tāṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyāmal, piṟariṭam colla veṭkappaṭṭuk koṇṭu poy pēci, perumaiyaṭittuk koḷḷukiṟa pētamaik kuṇamum vaḷara, vāyppai ēṟpaṭuttukiṟatu.



mēṟkūṟiya kuṟaikaḷ ellām taṟkālikamāṉatu tāṉ. takartteṟiyappaṭṭu, cari ceytu viṭak kūṭiyavaikaḷtāṉ.

takutiyāṉa talaimaiyuṭaṉ kaṭṭuppāṭu, maṉa uṟuti, ūkkam uḷḷavarkaḷāka māṇavarkaḷai vaḻi naṭattukiṟa pōtu, kuṟaikaḷ kaḷainteṟiyappaṭṭu viṭum.

nalla viḷaiyāṭṭu vīrarkaḷāka iruntāl kūṭa, tiṉantōṟum payiṟcikkum kūṭṭu muyaṟcikkum vantāl tāṉ, avarkaḷai kuḻuvil cērttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. appaṭi varukiṟa pōtu, tāṉ, oḻukkamum uyarnta paṇpu kaḷum vaḷarum. mēlē kūṟiya kuṟaikaḷ varāmalum irukkum.

ataṟkum mēlāka, nalla talaimai vēṇṭum. antat talaimai tavaṟiṉṟi vaḻikāṭṭum takkārākavum viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattum muṟaikaḷ puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷai nām mūṉṟu vakaiyil taṭattalām. 1. payirci pōṭṭikaḷ (Practice matches) 2. oru tuṟaip pōṭṭikaḷ (Closed competitions) 3. potu nilaip pōṭṭikaḷ (Open competitions)

1. payiṟcip pōṭṭikaḷ

tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa kumuviṉ tērccikkum, tiṟamai vaḷarccikkum utava, aṇṭai niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kuḻukkaḷai aḻaittu, pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷai āṭac ceytu, paḻakum muṟaiyē itu.

itaṟku muṉ kūṭṭiyē pēci, tēti, nēram, iṭam mutaliyavaṟṟaik kuṟittukkoṇṭu, tīrmāṉittu naṭattik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. oru tuṟaip pōṭṭikaḷ (Closed Competitions). oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟaiyaic cērnta, pirivaic cārnta kalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkiṭaiyē naṭaipeṟukiṉṟa pōṭṭikaḷ. (u.m) kallūrikaḷukkiṭaiyē (Inter Collegiate) paḷḷikaḷukkiṭaiyē (Inter School) maṭṭum naṭattappaṭutal. kallūrikaḷukkiṭaiyē paḷḷikaḷukkiṭaiyē eṉkiṟapōtu, vēṟu tuṟai cārnta niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ vantu paṅkēṟka muṭiyātu. atuvum kaṭṭuppaṭuttum poṟuppēṟṟuḷḷa caṅkaṅkaḷ mūlamāka naṭattappaṭum.

oru māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷ aṉaittum, anta māvaṭṭappaḷḷikaḷ uyarkalvik kaḻakattiṉ kaṭṭup pāṭṭil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru paḷḷiyum antak kaḻakattiṉ uṟuppiṉarkaḷ.

ataṉ nirvākakkuḻu, oru talaivar, ceyalar poruḷāḷar, tuṇaic ceyalar, ceyaṟkuḻu eṉpatāka amaintirukkum.

oru māvaṭṭam pala tokutikaḷāka (zones) pirikkappaṭṭu, ovvoru tokutiyaic cērnta paḷḷikaḷ, avaṟṟiṟkiṭaiyē pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattum. piṟaku, tokuti: veṟṟik kuḻukkaḷiṭaiyē pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattum. piṟakē, atu māvaṭṭap pōṭṭiyāka naṭattum.

nēramum, paṇa vacatiyum kiṭaittāl, māvaṭṭaṅkaḷukkiṭaiyēyum pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattum.

﻿

itupōlavē kallūrikaḷukku iṭaiyē, vaṅkikaḷukku iṭaiyē, aracu tuṟai niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkiṭaiyē, toḻilakat tuṟaiyiṉarukku iṭaiyē pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattappaṭum.

3. potu nilai pōṭṭikaḷ (Open Competitions) yār vēṇṭumāṉālum vantu pōṭṭiyil kalantu koḷḷalām eṉpatait tāṉ, potu nilaip pōṭṭikaḷ eṉkiṟōm. oru mānila viḷaiyāṭṭuk kaḻakam eṉpatu taṉatu viḷaiyāṭṭukkāṉa pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattukiṟapōtu, anta mānilattaic cērnta entak kuḻuvum vantu pōṭṭiyiṭalām.

ituvē potu nilaip pōṭṭiyākum,





﻿15 ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷ





(SPORTS MEET)



* * *



ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷai, taṉittiṟaṉ pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟum, viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ. nām ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟē kūṟuvōm.

intap pōṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭu vakaippaṭum.

1. taramāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ (Standard Meet)

2. taramaṟṟa pōṭṭikaḷ (Non-standard Meet)



taramāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ eṉpatu, akila ulaka ameccūr kaḻakam amaittut tantuḷḷa vitikaḷiṉpaṭiyē naṭaipeṟuvatākum.

taramaṟṟa pōṭṭikaḷ eṉpatu, antanta vaṭṭārattiṉarukku ēṟpa, vacatikkēṟpa, iṭattiṟkēṟpa amaittuk koḷvatākum.

1. taramāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ

ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattukiṟa viti muṟaikaḷ

oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattuvataṟku, mikavum tērcciyāṉa tiṭṭamum, tayārippum tēvai. ārvamum aṉupavamum niṟainta palariṉ ottuḻaippum tēvai.

tiṭṭam tayārittal : tērnta āṭkaḷai paṇiyil iyakkutal pōṉṟa ceyal muṟaikaḷai, iṅkē tokuttuk kāṇpōm. ātalāl, nām itaṉai 3 vakaiyākap pakuttup pārppōm.

1. pōṭṭikaḷukku muṉpuḷḷa paṇikaḷ (Pre-meetwork)

2. pōṭṭikaḷiṉpōtu ceyya vēṇṭiya vēlaikaḷ (Meet work)

3. pōṭṭikkup piṟaku mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya Gauss, so 5 sir (Post meet work)

1. pōṭṭikaḷukku muṉpuḷḷa paṇikaḷ

ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattuvataṟkeṉṟu, nirvākakkuḻu niccayamākat tēvaippaṭukiṟatu. (Organising Committee).

talaimaiyiṭamākak tikaḻum nirvākakkuḻu, pala tuṇai ceyaṟkuḻukkaḷai (Sub-Committee) amaikka vēṇṭum.

tuṇaik kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum kaṭamaikaḷum 1. 1. viḷamparak kuḻu (Publicity Committee)

pōṭṭikaḷ naṭaipeṟum tēti, iṭam, pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ pōṉṟa mukkiya kuṟippukkaḷai intak kuḻu viḷamparappaṭuttum pattirikkai, rēṭiyō, pōṭṭi niral cīṭṭukaḷ mūlamum itu ellōrukkum aṟivikkiṉṟa paṇiyai, ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu.

3.2. ōṭukaḷattarai maṟṟum utavi cātaṉak kuḻu Committee for the Grounds and equipments:

pōṭṭikaḷukkāṉa maitāṉattaik kēṭṭu, aṉumati vāṅki nikaḻccikaḷ naṭattum iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟiyīṭukaḷai ﻿amaittu, vēṇṭiya viḷaiyāṭṭu utavip poruṭkaḷai cērttu, cumukamāka taṭaiyiṉṟi pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattum ceyalkaḷai mēṟkoḷkiṟatu.

3.3. atikārikaḷai niyamikkum kuḻu (Committee for the officials)

pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattum tiṟamai peṟṟirukkum, atikārikaḷukku eḻuti, avarkaḷatu opputalaippeṟṟuk koṇṭu, atikārikaḷ aṭaṅkiya paṭṭiyalaiyum intak kuḻu tayārikka vēṇṭum.

nirvākak kuḻuviṉ aṉumatiyuṭaṉ, talaimai atikāriyaiyum niyamikka vēṇṭum.

3.1 irukkaikaḷ, taṅkum vacatikaḷ amaittuttarum kuḻu. (Committee for accommodation and seating arrangements)

veḷiyūriliruntu varukiṟa pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ, atikārikaḷ aṉaivarukkum taṅkum vacatikaḷai ceytu tarutal; viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭi viḻā naṭaipeṟum pantal, uṭkārum irukkai vacatikaḷ; pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ taṅki oyvukoḷḷum iṭaṅkaḷ, pārvaiyāḷarkaḷ, viruntiṉarkaḷ amarntirukkum iṭavacatikaḷ; kārkaḷ maṟṟum vākaṉaṅkaḷ niṟka vacatikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai, intakkuḻu ceytu taravēṇṭum.

3.5 varavēṟpuk kuḻu (Committee for reception)

pōṭṭikaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟapōtu, aḻaikkappaṭṭirukkum ciṟappu viruntiṉarkaḷ, mukkiya piramukarkaḷai varavēṟṟu, avarkaḷukkeṉṟu otukkappaṭṭirukkum irukkaikaḷil amarttum paṇiyaik kaikkoḷḷum kuḻuvāka itu amaintirukkiṟatu.



3-6. ōṭukaḷattiṉ alaṅkāram maṟṟum vaipavakkuḻu.

kaṇkaḷukku kavarcciyākavum pacumaiyākavum pantaya maitāṉam tōṟṟam taruvatu pōṉṟu alaṅkāram ceytal, pantayat toṭakkaviḻā, veṟṟip paricaḷippu viḻā, iṟuti viḻā pōṉṟavai ciṟappāka naṭaipeṟuvataṟkāka, kāriyamāṟṟutal, vekumatikaḷ veṟṟik kōppaikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟku ēṟpāṭu ceyyum poṟuppum, intak kuḻuvaic cārntatēyākum.

3 7. ciṟṟuṇṭi, maṟṟum kalai nikaḻcci amaippukkuḻu. (Committee for Refreshment and entertainment)

pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ, atikārikaḷ , ciṟappu viruntiṉarkaḷ, mukkiya piramukarkaḷ pōṉṟōrukku avvappōtu parukapāṉam, ciṟṟuṇṭiyum vaḻaṅkutal. pōṭṭi niṟai vuṟukiṟa nērattil paṭṭu āṭṭam pōṉṟa kalai nikaḻccikaḷai ēṟpāṭu ceyvatum intak kuḻuviṉ poṟuppāka amaikiṟatu.

38. peyarppativu, pōṭṭi niral tayārikkum kuḻu. (Committee for Entries and Programmes)

intak kuḻuvāṉatu, nuḻaivu maṉuvai, muṉkūṭṭiyē pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷukku aṉuppi, avarkaḷiṉ opputalai nērattil peṟṟu, pativu ceytu avarkaḷukkāṉa oṭṭa eṇkaḷ (Numbers) otukki, avaṟṟiṟkāṉa pativēṭṭil eḻuti nirappi, kuṟippiṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa nērattil, taṭaiyiṉṟi naṭakka ēṟpāṭu ceykiṟa poṟuppai ēṟṟirukkiṟatu.

ittakaiya poṟuppukkaḷai, kīḻkkāṇum muṟaikaḷil ceyyalām nikaḻcci niral (Programmes) puttakattil.

1. mēl aṭṭaiyil pōṭ kaṭattum niṟuvaṉattiṉ peyar, viḻāvukku varukiṟa muṟaiyē viruntiṉariṉ peyar pōṭṭikaḷ naṭaipeṟum iṭam, nāḷ, nēram mutaliya vivaraṅkaḷaik kuṟikkavēṇṭum.

2. palvēṟupaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷ, tuṇaik kuḻukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, kuḻukkaḷil iṭam peṟṟiruppōriṉ peyarkaḷaiyum kuṟittiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. pōṭṭikaḷai naṭattukiṟa atikārikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, avarkaḷ poṟuppēṟṟirukkum tuṟai paṟṟiyum kuṟittiṭa vēṇṭum.

4. pōṭṭiyil paṅkēṟkiṟa kuḻukkaḷai (Clubs) akaravaricaippaṭutti, pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyum tirappiṭa vēṇṭum.

5. pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ varicaiyai (order of events). kuṟittu, eṉṉeṉṉa nikaḻccikaḷ, ententa nērattil naṭaipeṟum eṉpataik kuṟittuk kāṭṭutal.

6. ovvoru pōṭṭi nikaḻcciyilum ēṟpaṭuttam paṭṭirukkum muntaiya cātaṉaikaḷ (Records).

7. muḻumaiyāka nirappu taṟkuriya veṟṟi eṇ kuṟippēṭu.

pōṭṭi kikaḻccikaḷ varicaiyai tayārikkum muṟai

1. oṭṭap pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟāl, taṭaitāṇṭum pōṭṭi, viraivōṭṭam, neṭuntura ōṭṭaṅkaḷ, toṭarōṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟu varicaippaṭutti, tayārikka vēṇṭum.

2. oṭṭappōṭṭikaḷ naṭaipeṟṟuk koṇṭirukkum. nērattilēyē, kaḷappōṭṭikaḷum aṭuttaṭuttu naṭattiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. viraivōṭṭakkārarkaḷ ellōrum, tāṇṭum pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpatāl irumpukkuṇṭu eṟiyāḷarkaḷ, taṭṭeṟital, caṅkilik kuṇṭu vīcutal pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷil, paṅkupeṟukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpatāl, ippaṭippaṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷai orē camayattiv nikaḻccikaḷ varicaiyil cērkkāmal, nēram koṭuttu varicaip paṭutta vēṇṭum.

4. kōlūṉṟittāṇṭum pōṭṭi, uyaram tāṇṭutal, taṭṭeṟital, vēleṟital pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷ naṭantu muṭiya atika nēram piṭikkum eṉpatāl, ittakaiya nikaḻccikaḷai, muṉkūṭṭiyē ārampittu naṭattappaṭuvatu pōla, nikaḻcci niralil cērttuka koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. pōṭṭi naṭaipeṟum pōtuḷḷa vēlaikaḷ (Meet Work)

1. pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟukiṉṟa pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ : naṭattukiṉṟa atikārikaḷ aṉaivarum, pōṭṭi toṭaṅkukiṉṟa nērattiṟku caṟṟu muṉṉatākavē vanta viṭa vēṇṭum.

2. atikārikaḷukkuriya aṭaiyāḷa aṭṭaikaḷ, pōṭṭiyaik kāṭṭum nikaḻcci niral piratikaḷ, kuṟippiṭṭa nikaḻccikkāṉa kuṟippēṭukaḷ, aṉaittaiyum koṭuttiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷukkuriya eṇkaḷ, (Numbers) : nikaḻcci niral piratikaḷ, maṟṟum avarkaḷukkuriya vaḻikāṭṭum aṟivuraik kuṟippukaḷ aṉaittaiyum tantu viṭa vēṇṭum.

4. pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ aṇivakuppu, uṟuti moḻi eṭuttal pōṉṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ pōṭṭikaḷait toṭaṅkiṭa vēṇṭum.

5. nikaḻcki niral varicaikku eṟpa, nikaḻccikaḷait toṭarntu naṭattavēṇṭum.

6. ovvoru pōṭṭiyiṉ iṟutippōṭṭi muṭintatum, veṟṟi mēṭai, paricaḷippu nikaḻcciyai uṭaṉē naṭattiṭa vēṇṭum.

7. pōṭṭinikaḻccikaḷ ellām muṭintapiṟaku, ellā pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷum, viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭiyiṉ muṭivu viḻāvukkākak kuṟippiṭṭirukkum iṭattil vantu kūṭiṭa vēṇṭum.

8. muṭivu viḻāvil, veṟṟik kēṭayaṅkaḷ, niṉaivup paricukaḷ, cāṉṟitaḻkaḷ mutaliyavai vaḻaṅkappaṭum.

9. piṟaku, nāṭṭuppaṇ pāṭa viḻā iṉitē muṭivu peṟum.

3. pōṭṭikkup piṟakuḷḷa vēlaikaḷ 1. viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ muṭinta piṟaku, kaṇakku vaḻakkaic cariceytu viṭa vēṇṭum. maṟṟavarkaḷiṭamiruntu kaimāṟṟāka vāṅki vantap poruṭkaḷaik koṇṭu pōy koṭukka vēṇṭum.

2. pōṭṭikaḷukku utavi ceyta aṉaivarukrum, naṉṟi terivittuk kaṭitam eḻuta vēṇṭum. nēril colvatum nallatutāṉ.

11 taramaṟṟa pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ ulaka vitikaḷukku uṭpaṭāmal, vacatikkēṟpa naṭattukiṉṟa pōṭṭikaḷait tāṉ, taramaṟṟa viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ eṉkiṟōm.

ulaka vitimuṟaikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟāmal, naṭaimuṟaikku utavumāṟu pala putiya vitikaḷai ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭu, atika eṇṇikkaiyil (māṇavarkaḷai) pōṭṭiyāḷar kaḷaip paṅku peṟac ceyyum utti, inta viḷaiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷil, kaṭaipiṭikkappaṭukiṟatu.

irukkiṉṟa koñca vacatikaḷē, intap pōṭṭikku pōtumāṉatu. itil veṟṟi peṟukiṟavarkaḷiṉ cātaṉaikku avvaḷavu peṟumitamillai, eṉṟālum, kuḻukkaḷukkup perumai pōyc cērukiṟatu. avvaḷavu tāṉ.

intap pōṭṭiyil iṭampeṟum cila pirivukaḷ.

1. kuḻukkaḷiṉ kūṭṭup pōṭṭi (Team or mass Athletics)

2. calukaip pōṭṭikaḷ (Handicap sports)

3. takaval vaḻi pōṭṭikaḷ (Telegraphic sports)

4. tiṟamaṟiyum pōṭṭikaḷ (Tabloid sports)

kuḻukkaḷum kūṭṭuppōṭṭiyum

atika eṇṇikkaiyil māṇavarkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṟa pōtu, oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷai tiṭṭamiṭṭu naṭatta vēṇṭum.

1. māṇavarkaḷai ciṟu ciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirikka vēṇṭum. oru kuḻu eṉṟāl 20 mutal 25 irukkalām.

2. cila pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷil avarkaḷaip pōṭṭi yiṭac ceyyalām.

ōṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷ (a) oru kuḻuvil uḷḷavarkaḷ ovvoruvarum oru vaṭṭam (lap) cuṟṟi ōṭi vara, oruvar piṉ oruvarākac ceytal. itil entakkuḻu kuṟainta nērattil ōṭi muṭikkiṟatō, atuvē veṉṟatākum. itaṟku toṭar muṟai (Follow on method) eṉṟu peyar.

ā) toṭarōṭṭa (Relay) pōla, etiretirē māṇavarkaḷaip pirittu niṟkac ceytu (100 mīṭṭar tāram). taṟi ōṭṭam pōla. (Shuttle Relay) oṭiṭac ceytal. itaṟku taṟimuṟai (Shuttle method) eṉṟu peyar.

i) ovvoru māṇavaṉum kuṟippiṭṭa tūrattai (85 mīṭṭar tūram) oṭac ceytu, entak kuḻu kuṟainta nērattil mottattil, oṭi muṭikkiṟatō, antak kuḻu veṉṟatāka aṟivittu, ataṟkāṉa veṟṟi eṇkaḷai aḷikka vēṇṭum. (5, 3, 2.) eṉpatu pōla) itaṟku kuḻup pōṭṭi muṟai (Zonal method) eṉṟu peyar.

kaḷa nikaḻccikaḷ nīḷam tāṇṭutal, taṭṭeṟital, irumpukkuṇṭu pōṭutal pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷil, kuḻukkaḷaip paṅku peṟac ceytu, atika tūram tāṇṭukiṟa, atika tūram eṟikiṟa kuḻukkaḷukku, veṟṟip pariciṉai aḷikkalām.

kaḷap pōṭṭikaḷum itē pōla, toṭar muṟai, taṟi muṟai, kuḻup pōṭṭi muṟai mūṉṟaiyum kaṭaip piṭikkalām.

2. tiṟaṉ kuṟaintōrkku, calukaip pōṭṭikaḷ tiṟaṉ kuṟaintōraiyum, calukai muṟaiyil vāyppaḷittu, camamākap pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟa muṟai, ippōṭṭikaḷil piṉpaṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.

(u. m) 100 mīṭṭar oṭṭap pōṭṭi eṉṟāl, tiṟaṉ kuṟaintavarkaḷai, 10 aṭi allatu 20 aṭi tūram muṉṉē niṟka vaittu, oṭac ceytu, pōṭṭiyiṭa vaippatākum.

tiṟaṉ kuṟaintavarkku oru vāyppu tarukiṟa calukaip pōṭṭiyēyākum.

3. takaval vaḻip pōṭṭikaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷil, ūrkaḷil pōṭṭikaḷai naṭatti, anta muṭivukaḷai tanti, ṭelipōṉ mūlamākat takaval tara vēṇṭum; anta cātaṉaikaḷukkēṟpa, muṭivukaḷ aṟivikkappaṭum.

itil oru kuṟai eṉṉaveṉil, pala iṭaṅkaḷil pōṭṭikaḷ eṉkiṟa pōtu, antanta iṭam cūḻnilai kāraṇamāka, cātaṉaikaḷ vēṟupaṭum, tiṟamaikaḷai veḷippaṭutta muṭiyāta taṉmaiyum amaiyum eṉpatu maṟṟapaṭi, inta muṟaip pōṭṭiyiṉāl nēram vīṇākāmal, paṇac celavu atikamākāmal miccam piṭikkalām. kāppāṟṟalām.

4. tiṟaṉaṟiyum pōṭṭikaḷ (Tabloid Sports) inta muṟaip pōṭṭikaḷil kalantu koḷpavarkkāka, mutalilēyē, cātaṉai nērattaiyum tūrattaiyum (Time and distance) tīrmāṉamākak kuṟittu vaittu viṭa vēṇṭum.

taram I taram II taram III

(u.m)

100 mīṭṭar tūram 12.5 viṉāṭi 13 viṉāṭi 14 viṉāṭi

nīḷam tāṇṭal 15 aṭi 14 aṭi 13 aṭi

irumpukkuṇṭu eṟital (12) 30 aṭi 28 36

﻿ ovvoru tarattiṟkum ovvoru vaṇṇa rippaṉ eṉṟu terivittiṭa vēṇṭum.

kuṟippiṭṭa oru nērattil oṭi muṭippavarkku: turattil tāṇṭupavarkkuk, kuṟippiṭṭa rippaṉaik koṭuttu, keḷaravittiṭa vēṇṭum.

mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa 3 nikaḻccikaḷ, 3 taraṅkaḷ. itil ovvoru māṇavaṉum pōṭṭiyiṭṭu muyaṟcikkiṟāṉ. mūṉṟu nikaḻcciyilum kuṟippiṭṭa cātaṉaiyaik kaṭantāl tāṉ, rippaṉ tarappaṭum. oṉṟil kaṭakka muṭiyāmaṟ pōṉālum, rippaṉ kiṭaikkātu.

atika eṇṇikkaiyil rippaṉ peṟṟa (ovvoru pēpaṉukkum veṟṟi eṇ evvaḷavu eṉṟu kuṟit tirukka vēṇṭum.) kuḻukkaḷē, veṟṟikkuḻu eṉṟu aṟivikkappaṭum.

ivvāṟu atika eṇṇikkaiyil māṇavarkaḷaip paṅkupeṟac ceykiṟa pōtu : māṇavarkaḷai ciṟuvar, iḷaiyōr mūttavar eṉṟu pirittal; kuṟippiṭṭa pōṭṭikaḷil maṭṭum māṇavarkaḷaip pōṭṭiyiṭa vaittal; tiṟamuḷḷa māṇavarkaḷai cērkkāmal iruttal; calukaippōṭṭi muṟaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttutal; ellōrum kaṭṭāyamākap pōṭṭiyil kalantu koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu āṇaiyiṭutal; māṟuvēṭap pōṭṭi, pōṉṟavaṟṟai aṉumatittāl pōṉṟavaṟṟilum āciriyarkaḷ tīvira kavaṉam celutta vēṇṭum.





﻿16. viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻā





﻿(PLAY DAY)





* * *



paḷḷikaḷukkiṭaiyē naṭaipeṟukiṟa pōṭṭikaḷukkup patilāka, peṇkaḷukkeṉṟu, paḷḷikaḷukkuḷḷēyē naṭattappaṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷē viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷ eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟiruntaṉa. atuvē māṟi pakkam pakkamāka uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷ ellām vantu, kalantukoṇṭu, kaḷippaṭaikiṟa mārkkattiṟkākap piṉṉar, māṟṟam ceyyappaṭṭatu.

pala paḷḷikaḷ vantu kalantukoṇṭālum, itu pōṭṭikaṉ pōla naṭattappaṭuvatu alla.

kūṭi viḷaiyāṭuvatu tāṉ. etirttu viḷaiyāṭuvatu alla.

viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷiṉ kuṟikkōḷāṉatu, ovvoru māṇavaṉum ētāvatu oru viḷaiyāṭṭil avaciyam paṅku peṟṟāka vēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉ.

viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻāviṉ payaṉum perumaiyum

1. palatarappaṭṭa makiḻcci tarum viḷaiyāṭṭukkavil, peruvāriyāṉa eṇṇikkaiyil māṇava māṇavikaḷ kalantu koṇṭu, pēriṉpam peṟa utavukiṉṟatu.

2. camūkattoṭarpu koḷḷum vāyppaiyum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

3. talaimaip paṇpai vaḷarttukkoḷḷum cantarp paṅkaḷai aḷikkiṟatu. 4. puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷil ēṟpaṭukiṟa kuṟaikaḷaik kaḷaintu, kutūkalam peṟum nalaṉai ēṟpaṭuttit tarukiṟatu.

viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻāvai naṭattum muṟaikaḷ

1. paḷḷik kuḻantaikaḷukkākavē naṭattappaṭukiṟa nikaḻcciyātalāl, itaṉai oru nāḷilō allatu nāḷ pakutikkuḷḷō naṭattalām.

2. nikaḻccikaḷai naṭatta, pala kuḻukkaḷai amaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum (u. m.) viḷamparkkuḻu : nikaḻcci niral kuḻu varavēṟpuk kuḻu vacatikkuḻu : viḷaiyāṭṭu poruṭkaḷ kuḻu ; ciṟṟuṇṭikkuḻu ciṟappuk koṇṭāṭṭakkuḻu eṉṟu palavakaikkuḻukkaḷ.

3. viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻā naṭaipeṟum nāḷ, iṭam, nēram, piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭiya nikaḻcci niral pōṉṟa oṟṟai. paḷḷikaḷukku muṉkaṭṭiyē takaval tantu, ovvoru paḷḷiyiliruntum varukiṟa kuḻantaikaḷ eṇṇikkaiyaiyum peṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. -

4. varukiṟa kuḻantaikaḷ eṇṇikkaikkēṟpa taṅkum iṭavacati kaḻivaṟaikaḷ vacati : uṇavu vacati, taṇṇir ācati, pōṉṟavaṟ niṟkum ciṟappāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷaic ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

5. eṉṉeṉṉa nikaḻccikaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpataṟkēṟpa, āṭukaḷaṅkaḷaittayār ceytu vaittu, ataṟkuriya utavip poruṭkaḷaiyum niṟaivāka ēṟpāṭu ceytu vaittal vēṇṭum.

6. paḷḷikaḷiliruntu varum kuḻantaikaḷai, pala kuḻukkaḷākap pirittu, avarkaḷ māṟi māṟic ceṉṟu viḷaiyāṭṭil paṅkupeṟum vaḻi muṟaikaḷaiyum, avarkaḷukkut teḷivāka viḷakkutal vēṇṭum.

7. ellā kuḻukkaḷukkum camavāyppu, niṟaivāṉa vāyppukaḷ kiṭaikkumpaṭi, kavaṉamākavum poṟuppākavum pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

8. nikaḻcci niralil, ciṟuciṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷa, kōpurap payiṟcikaḷ lecim pōṉṟa tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷ, cākacac ceyalkaḷ pōṉṟa makiḻccitarum ceyalkaḷ iṭampeṟukiṟa muṟaiyil irukkavēṇṭum.

9. nikaḻccikaḷukkiṭaiyē pāṉaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkutal nērattiṟku uṇavu koṭuttal pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum cariyākat tarutal vēṇṭum.

10. nikaḻccikaḷ ellām naṭaipeṟṟa piṟaku ellōraiyum oriṭattil kūṭacceytu, avarkaḷ taṅkaḷatu tiṟamaikaḷaik kāṭṭukiṟa cantarppam aḷikkavēṇṭum (āṭal, pāṭal, oppaṉai, māṟuvēṭappōṭṭi, ciṟu ciṟu nāṭakam pōṉṟavai).

11. ovvoru paḷḷiyum tamatu kuḻantaikaḷ mūlam alai nikaḻcciyai aḷikkavum vāyppukkaḷ taralām.

12. ippaṭi ceyalviḷakkam, ciṟappu nikaḻccikaḷ iṭam peṟṟa piṟaku, muṭivu viḻā oṉṟiṟkum ēṟpāṭu ceytal vēṇṭum.

13. viḻāvukkut talaimai ēṟka oruvar, varavēṟpurai, aṟikkai vācittup paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkutal, naṉṟi. navilal, nāṭṭuppaṇ ippaṭiyāka muṭivu viḻā naṭaipeṟa ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytal vēṇṭum.

14. ellā nikaḻccikaḷum kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkuḷ muṭivaṭaintu iruṭṭuvataṟku muṉ kuḻantaikaḷ vīṭu pōyc cērvatu pōla, viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻāvai niṟaivu ceytal vēṇṭum.

vacatikkāka, viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻā oṉṟiṉ nikaḻcci niralpaṟṟi, kīḻē koṭuttirukkiṟōm.

viḷaiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻā nikaḻcci kiral (mātiri)



nāḷ : 18-8-1989.

nēram : mālai 2 maṇi mutal 6 maṇi varai.

iṭam : oy. em. ci. ē. uṭaṟkalvik kallūri.

nikaḻcci :

1. paḷḷik kuḻantaikaḷiṉ kuḻu kūṭutalum, aṇi vakuppum.

2. koṭiyēṟṟutal-tiṟappu viḻā.

3. viḻā nāḷ paṟṟiya viḷakkamum kuṟippum.

4. viḷaiyāṭṭu nikaḻccikaḷ.





(a) tāḷalayac ceyalkaḷ.



(ā) cākacac ceyalkaḷ.



(i) ciṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ.



(ī) pāṭṭum kataiyum iṇainta ceyalkaḷ.



5. iṭaivēḷai-ciṟṟuṇṭi nēram.

6. ovvoru paḷḷiyum paṅkupeṟum nikaḻccikaḷ ceyal viḷakkam, ciṟappup payiṟcikaḷ.

7. mēṭai muṉ kūṭutal : māṇavarkaḷiṉ kalai nikaḻccikaḷ.

8. ciṟappuk kūṭṭam (muṭivu viḻā).



(a) varavēṟpurai.



(ā) viḻā aṟikkai vācittal.



(i) talaivar ciṟappurai.



(ī) paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkutal.



(u) naṉṟi urai.



9. nāṭṭup paṇ.





17. ceyal māṭciyum kaṇkāṭciyum





﻿ (DEMONSTRATIONS AND EXIBITION)





* * *



paḷḷiyil naṭaipeṟukiṉṟa mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷ allatu viḻākkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil naṭattappeṟukiṉṟa uṭal payiṟcikaḷai ceytu kāṭṭukiṉṟa māṭciyaiyē ceyal: māṭci eṉṟu aḻaikkiṟōm.

ceyal māṭciyiṉ ciṟappu nōkkaṅkaḷ

1. māṇavarkaḷukku ciṟappāṉa muṟaiyil, palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa uṭaṟ payiṟcikaḷaip payiṟṟuvikkum vāyppu: kiṭaikkiṟatu.

2. uṭaṟ kalviyil māṇavarkaḷukku uṇmaiyāṉa viruppamum, uṟutiyāṉa ārvamum ēṟpaṭuttum, vaḻivakai kiṭaikkiṟatu.

3. cemmaiyāka ceyalpaṭavum, taṅkaḷ tiṟamaikaḷai veḷippaṭutti maṟṟavarkaḷai makiḻvittu, tāṅkaḷum makiḻntuviṭukiṟa vāyppu, uṭaṟkalvi āciriyarkaḷukkum kiṭaikkiṟatu.

4. paḷḷi nikaḻccikkup pārvaiyāḷarkaḷāka varuāai tarukiṉṟa peṟṟōrkaḷ, potumakkaḷukku, uṭaṟkalviyaippaṟṟi terintu koḷḷavum, purintu koḷḷavum kūṭiya uṉṉata cūḻnilaiyai uṇṭākkit tarukiṟatu.

paḷḷiyil varum mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷ

1. tiruviḻā nāṭkaḷ.

2. paḷḷi niṟuvaṉar viḻā.

3. peṟṟōrkaḷ viḻā.

4. ciṟappu viruntiṉar varukai viḻā.

5. paḷḷi āṇṭu viḻā.

6. muṉṉāḷ māṇavar caṅka viḻā.

7. tēciya tiṉa viḻākkaḷ.

8. etir pārāmal varukiṟa mukkiya viḻākkaḷ.



naṭattukiṉṟa muṟaikaḷ

1. kūṭṭup payiṟcikaḷukkuriya ceyal māṭcik kāṭcil kāka, muṉkūṭṭiyē tiṭṭamiṭṭuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum, ataṟku ētuvāka, nāḷ, nēram iṭam, kālam pōṉṟa vaṟṟaiyum tīrmāṉittu viṭa vēṇṭum.

2. nikaḻcci naṭappatai peṟṟōr, potumakkaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷum, muṭinta vaḻikaḷil ellām, viḷamparam ceytiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. eṉṉeṉṉa uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷaik kūṭṭuppayiṟci yil cērkka vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum tiṭṭamiṭṭu, tīrmāṉikka vēṇṭum.

4. ententak kuḻu, eṉṉeṉṉap payiṟcikaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum cariyāka, teḷivāka, uṟuti ceyya vēṇṭum.

5. taṉittaṉiyāka ovvoru kuḻuvaiyum, ataṟkuriya kayiṟcikaḷai aḷittu, piṟaku, ellā kuḻukkaḷaiyum oṉṟu cērttu ceykiṟa ceyal māṭcikku, palamuṟai ottikaikaḷ tēvai.

6. enta iṭattil, ceyal māṭci nikaḻcci naṭai peṟumō, anta iṭattilēyē ottikai pārppatum illatu.

7. viḻāvukkāṉa ciṟappu viruntiṉaraiyum mu ṉ kūṭṭiyē teḷivu ceytu, aḻaippitaḻukkum ēṟpāṭu ceyya vēṇṭum.

8. nuḻaivu vāyil, veḷiyē cellum vaḻi pōṉṟa vaṟṟai amaittu, pārvaiyāḷarkaḷ vacatiyāka vantu, iruntu, pōka ēṟpāṭu ceyvatu nallatu.

ceyalmāṭci naṭaipeṟum nāḷukku muṉṉatāka, mēlē kūṟiya vivaraṅkaḷaik kaṭaippiṭitta piṟaku, viḻā nikaḻccikkāka atē nāḷil ceyya vēṇṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷ.

1. pārvaiyāḷarkaḷukkāka irukkaikaḷ. mēṭai alaṅkāraṅkaḷ.

2. nikaḻcci naṭaipeṟum iṭattil kuṟiyīṭukaḷ.

3. nikaḻccikkut tēvaiyāṉa utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷait tayārāka cēkarittu vaittiruttal.

4. paṅkēṟkum māṇavarkaḷukkuriya cīruṭaikaḷ.

5. paṅkēṟkum māṇavarkaḷ taṅki, uṭaiyai cīci ceyya iṭa vacatikaḷ.

6. oli, oḷi amaippu.

7. kuṭi nīr vacati, kaḻivaṟai vacatikaḷ.

8. mutalutavi vacatikaḷ.

ivaiyellām, mukkiyamāka kavaṉikkappaṭavēṇṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷākum.

ceyalmāṭcikkuriya nikaḻcci niralait tayāritta piṟaku, avaṟṟaip paṟṟiya virivurai viḷakkamum taral vēṇṭum.

ovvoru nikaḻcci paṟṟiya virivurai viḷakkamāṉatu. oru nikaḻcciyait toṭarntu maṟṟoru nikaḻcciyait toṭarntu koḷḷavum, pārvaiyāḷarkaḷukku nikaḻcci paṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷavum utavi ceykiṟatu.

ceyal māṭci nikaḻcciyai kuṟitta nērattiṟkuḷ, (6.5kima uṅkaḷukkuḷ) muṭittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. atika nēram eṭuttuk koḷvatu, pārvaiyāḷarkaḷukku ericcalai ēṟpaṭuttiṉālum ēṟpaṭuttum.

tēciya kītam icaittu, viḻāvai muṭittiṭa vēṇṭum.

ceyal māṭcikkuriya ceyal muṟaikaḷ

1. cīruṭaṟpayircikaḷ (Calisthenics)

2. utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ceykiṟa uṭalpayiṟcikaḷ.

3. aṇi naṭaippayiṟcikaḷ

4. tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷ (immi, kōlāṭṭam pōṉṟavai)

5. intiya nāṭṭut tēkappayiṟcikaḷ (taṇṭāl, paski)

6. kuṭṭikkaraṇap payiṟcikaḷ.

7. jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷ.

8. taṟkāppukkalaip payiṟcikaḷ.

9. oṭukaḷappōṭṭikaḷ, viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷ paṟṟi cittarikkum cilaikkāṭci amaippu (Tableaux) kaṇkāṭci.

aṟivu viḷakkamum, ārvamum aḷikkak kūṭiya vakaiyil, kaṇkāṭciyai amaikka vēṇṭum.

viḷaiyāṭṭu, uṭal nalam, uṭal payiṟcikaḷ pōṉṟa talaippukkaḷil, paṭaṅkaḷ, oviyaṅkaḷ, mātiriccīlaikaḷ, kalaroṭṭikaḷ mūlamāka kaṇkāṭciyai amaikkalām.

pārvaiyāḷarkaḷukkut teḷivāka viḷakka, māṇavarkaḷai payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.

kaṇkāṭcik keṉṟu taṉi iṭam vēṇṭum. vantu pōkum vaḻikaḷum tārāḷamāka irukkavēṇṭum.

kaṇkāṭciyai naṭatta, poṟuppuḷḷa naṭuvarai talaimaiyēṟkac ceytu, avar talaimaiyiṉ kīḻ, oru kuḻuvai amaittu viṭukiṟapōtu, kaṇkāṭci ciṟappu mikkatāka, payaṉaḷippatāka amaintu, perumai cērkkum.





﻿18. viḷaiyāṭṭuc cuṟṟulā





(GAMES TOURS)





* * *



paḷḷikaḷil allatu kallūrikaḷil tāṉ, māṇavar kaḷukkāṉa viḷaiyāṭṭuc cuṟṟulāvai, ēṟpāṭu ceytu, naṭattiṭa muṭiyum.

cuṟṟulāvukkāṉa paṇac celavu maṟṟum viṭumuṟai nāṭkaḷ paṟṟi, muṉkūṭṭiyē tiṭṭamiṭṭu naṭattukiṟa pōtu tāṉ, cuṟṟulā cukamākavum, cumukamākavum amaiyum.

cuṟṟulā veṟṟikku, cila kuṟippukkaḷ

1. cuṟṟulāvukku enta iṭattiṟkup pōkiṟōm enta nāḷil puṟappaṭukiṟōm ? evvaḷavu nāṭkaḷ evvaḷavu paṇam celavākum eṉṟellām mutalil tiṭṭamiṭal vēṇṭum.

2. celavu muḻuvataiyum paḷḷi ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟatā ? pakuti celavai ēṟkiṟatā ? māṇavarkaḷē muḻumaiyāka ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟārkaḷā eṉpataiyellām, muṉṉatākavē, tiṭṭamiṭṭut tīrmāṉikka vēṇṭum.



8. pas allatu rayil mūlam payaṇam ceyvatu eṉṟāl, ataṟkuriya calukaik kaṭṭaṇam pōṉṟa vaṟṟiṟku maṉu ceytu, ēṟpāṭu ceytuviṭal vēṇṭum.

4. cuṟṟulā cellum iṭaṅkaḷukku, muṉ kūṭṭiyē uriyavarkaḷukku eḻuti, taṅkum vacati, uṇavu mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku ēṟpāṭukaḷai ceytuviṭa vēṇṭum.

5. puṟappaṭum nāḷ, nēram, cellum iṭaṅkaḷ. ovvoru iṭattiliruntum puṟappaṭum nēram. payaṇattiṟ kuriya pas allatu rayil.nēram mutaliyavaṟṟait tayārikkum tikaḻccik kuṟippup paṭṭiyalaiyum tayārittu, ataṉ paṭiyē tāmatappaṭuttāmal, oḻuṅkāka naṭattiṭa vēṇṭum.

6. cuṟṟulāvukku paṇam tāṉ mukkiyam. kaiyil tēvaikku atikamākap paṇam vaittiruppatu, putiya iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṟa etirpārāta tontaravukaḷai camāḷikka utavum.

7. putiya iṭaṅkaḷukkup pōvatāl, paṇattaip pattiramāka vaittuk koḷvatu putticālittaṉamākum. atupōlavē, celavaḻikkum paṇattiṟkuriya racītukaḷaiyum vāṅki vaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. paṇa viṣayattil mikavum poṟuppu avaciyam.

8. cuṟṟulā nērattil, cukavīṉattiṟku palar āḷā vatu cakajamē. potuvāka, upayōkappaṭuttukiṟa cila mukkiya maruntukaḷaiyum, mutalutavi tarukiṟa maruntukaḷaiyum, vāṅki vaittiruppatu nallatu.

9. cuṟṟulā celpavarkaḷ makiḻcciyāṉa cūḻnilaiyil iruppatu iyaṟkaitāṉ. iruntālum avarkaḷ ellai mīṟi naṭantu koḷḷāmal, oḻukkattuṭaṉum kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉum irukka, cuṟṟulā poṟuppēṟṟiruk kum āciriyar, ciramattuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭiyirukkum.

eṉavē, makiḻcciyākavum, maṉōkaramākavum cuṟṟulā amaiya, āciriyarkaḷ māṇavarkaḷ kūṭṭu ottuḻaippu avaciyamākum.

ivai ellām tiṭṭamiṭṭu, tīrmāṉittu celkiṟa pōtu, tēvaikku mēlē ṇavacati irukkiṟapōtu, ciṟappāka amaiyum. atuvum poṟuppuṇarntu naṭantu koṉkiṟapōtu, pēriṉpamākavum amaintuviṭum eṉpatai uṟutiyākak kūṟalām.





﻿19. māṇavarkaḷiṉ iṉappirivum, paricum pārāṭṭum





(CLASSIFICATIONS & AWARDS)





* * *



iṉappirivu eṉpatu māṇavarkaḷai taram vāriyākap pirippatākum. vayatu, uṭal aḷavi (Size) amaippu, viḷaiyāṭṭut tiṟamai mutaliyavaṟṟai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, māṇavarkaḷaik kuḻu kuḻuvākap pirippatākum.

ciṟuvar, iḷaiyōr, mūttōr (Sub - Junior, Junior Senior, Super senior) eṉappirikkalām.

ivvāṟu pirippataṟku muṉ kavaṉikkavēṇṭiyatu.

1. nalla tēkattuṭaṉ, ellā viḷaiyāṭṭilum kalantu koḷḷa muṭikiṟa māṇavarkaḷ.

2. uṭal kuṟaiyuṭaṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa viḷaiyāṭ ṭukkaḷil maṭṭumē paṅku peṟa muṭiyum eṉkiṟa māṇavarkaḷ.

3. maruttuva āyviṉ aṭippaṭaiyil viḷaiyāṭṭil vilakkaḷikkappaṭṭa māṇavarkaḷ.

eṉṟu mutalil, 3 vakaiyākap pirittuk koḷḷalām. taramum tiṟamum uḷḷa māṇavarkaḷai iṉavāriyākac pirikka pala muṟaikaḷ kaiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

mutalmuṟai

vayatu + uyaram - eṭai

3

muṭivaṭainta vayatu, aṅkulattil uyaram, pavuṇṭil eṭai ākiya mūṉṟaiyum kūṭṭi, 3-āl vakukkavēṇṭum.

ippaṭi varukiṉṟa eṇṇikkaiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil: nāṉku pirivākap pirikkalām.

pirivu kūṭṭil varum eṇṇikkai

A 90kku mēl

B 85liruntu 9 varai

C 75 liruntu 85 varai

D 75kkuk kīḻāka

iraṇṭāvatu muṟai

ṭākṭar C. H. mekḷāyiṉ iṉappirivu muṟai

ivar oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟṟa māṇavarkaḷ palarai āyvu ceytu, oru muṭivukku vantār.

1. vayatu (Chronological age)

2. uṭaltiṟam (Physiological age)

3. uyaram (Height)

4. eṭai (Weight) inta nāṉkaiyum payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum vaḻi muṟaikaḷai vakuttu, oru cūttirattai (Formulla) aḷittār.

ivar koṭuttuḷḷa kaṇakkuppaṭi, 17 āṇṭukaḷ varai vayataiyum; 14 vayatukkup piṟaku uyarattaiyum; ellā vayatu maṭṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa eṭaiyaiyum iṉappirivukkāka eṭuttuk koḷḷalām eṉpatu tāṉ.

(a) kūṭṭu=29 vayatu + 6 uyaram + eṭai

17 āṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa vayataiyum 17 ākavē, koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

(ā) kūṭṭu=6 uyaram + eṭai

27 āṇṭu vayatum, ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vayatum uṭaiya kallūri māṇavarkaḷukku inta cūttirattaip payaṉpaṭuttalām.

(i) kūṭṭu= 10 vayatu + eṭai

inta vāyppāṭṭai toṭakkap paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām. intac ciṟārkaḷukku uyaram tēvaiyaṟṟatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.

mūṉṟāvatu muṟai

cūttiram 11/2 uyaram (aṅkulattil) + eṭai (pavuṇṭil).

inta cūttirattiṉpaṭi, varukiṟa motta eṇṇikkaiyaik koṇṭu. nāṉku pirivākap pirikkalām.

1. mika mūttōr - 200kku mēl

2. mūttōr - 180kku mēl 200kku kīḻ

3. iḷaiyōr - 160kkum 180kkum kīḻ

4. ciṟuvar - 180kkum kīḻāka

nākāṉkāvatu muṟai kūṭṭu = vayatu + uyaram + l/10 eṭai

naṭantu muṭinta āṇṭukaḷai vayatākavum aṅkulattil uyarattaiyum pavuṇṭil eṭaiyaiyum eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

appaṭi varukiṟa motta eṇṇikkaiyaik koṇṭu, kīḻvarum kaṇakkil, iṉappirivu ceyyalām.

mika mūttōr = 85 m ataṟku mēlum

mūttōr = 80 mutal 85 varai.

iḷaiyōr = 75 mutal 80 varai.

ciṟuvar =70 mutal 75 varai.

ārampa vakuppiṉar =65 mutal 70 varai.

kuḻantaikaḷ = 65kkum kīḻāka

utāraṇam

15 āṇṭukaḷ - 15

5 aṭi 2 aṅkulam - 6.2

80 pavuṇṭu eṭai - 8 (80 ÷ 10 = 8)

----

mottam 85

----

85 eṉṟu kūṭṭu varuvatāl, anta māṇavar mika mūttōr pirivil cērkiṟār.

peṇkaḷukkāṉa iṉappirivu

peṇkaḷ vaḷarcciyaik koṇṭu, 15 vayatukku mēṟpaṭṭa peṇkaḷaiyellām, orē pirivil cērttuk koḷḷalām.

15 vayatukkuṭpaṭṭavarkaḷai pirittu viṭukiṟa muṟai.

1. kūṭṭu = vayatu + uyaram + eṭai

2. kūṭṭu = vayatu + uyaram + eṭai

muṭinta āṇṭukaḷai vayatākavum, aṅkulattil uyarattaiyum, pavuṇṭil eṭaiyaiyum kaṇakkil koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

ivvāṟu pirippataṉ nōkkam, māṇavarkaḷaip pōṭṭiyil īṭupaṭutti, tiṟamaiyai vaḷarppataṟkēyākum.

pōṭṭiyil paṅku koḷpavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ tiṟamaiyaip paṟṟi terintu koḷvatuṭaṉ, maṟṟavarkaḷ tiṟamaikkum mēlākap pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, pōrāṭi veṟṟi peṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.

veṟṟikkup paricum pārāṭṭum, matippum mariyātaiyum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. pukaḻum perumita cātaṉaiyum kiṭaikkiṉṟatu.

paricukaḷaiyum pārāṭṭukkaḷaiyum vaḻaṅkuvatil oru marapai mēṟkoḷvatu avaciyamākiṉṟatallavā!

vilai matippuḷḷa paricup poruṭkaḷait tarukiṟa poḻutu, avaṟṟai viṟṟuviṭukiṉṟa maṉitarkaḷ niṟaiyatu pērkaṉ uḷḷaṉar.

ākavē, pariciṉ matippai paṇam koṇṭu aḷippatāl, appaṭippaṭṭa paricait tavirttu, piṟkālattil avai utavukiṉṟa vakaikaḷil taruvatu tāṉ putti cālittaṉamāṉa ceyalākum.

1. naṟcāṉṟitaḻkaḷ vaḻaṅkutal.

2. atika vilaiyaṟṟa paricup poruṭkaṉai vaḻaṅkutal.

3. veṉṟa kuḻuvukku veṟṟik kēṭayaṅkaḷ aḷittal.

4. matippup palakaiyil, veṟṟiyāḷarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai, cātaṉaiyuṭaṉ eḻuti vaittal.

5. veṟṟiyāḷarkaḷiṉ pukaip paṭattaiyum, matippup palakaiyil oṭṭi vaittal.

uṭaṟkalviyāṉatu uṭalai vaḷarkkavum, tiṟamaiyai mikutippaṭuttavum, muyaṟcikkiṉṟa maṉappāṅkai uyarttavum niṟaiya pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟavum, veṟṟi peṟavum kūṭiya vāyppukkaḷai vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

anta vāyppukkaṉai atikappaṭutta, ārvamūṭṭa ippaṭippaṭṭa paricukaḷum pārāṭṭukkaḷum peritum uttukiṉṟaṉa. uṟcākam ūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.





20. tērvu neṟikaḷum aḷavu muṟaikaḷum

(TESTS AND MEASUREMENTS)



* * *



tērvu neṟikaḷum, aḷavu muṟaikaḷum, māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟamai, tērccipaṟṟi, teḷivākat terintu koḷḷa utavukiṉṟaṉa.

tērvukaḷ ellām māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟamaikaḷ, valimai, nīṭittuḻaikkum āṟṟal, viḷaiyāṭṭu paṟṟiya aṟivu, naṭattai, paṇpāṟṟal paṟṟi terintu koḷḷa utavukiṉṟaṉa.

aḷavu muṟaikaḷ ellām, māṇavarkaḷiṉ uṭal aḷavu, amaippu, uyaram, eṭai, uṭal iyaṅku āṟṟal, cātaṉai paṟṟiyellām aṟintu koḷḷa utavukiṉṟaṉa.

tērvum aḷavum etaṟkāka?

1. māṇavarkaḷiṉ palattaiyum palahīṉattaiyum aṟintu koḷḷa.

2. māṇavarkaḷai ōriṉap pirivākappirittuk koḷḷa.

3. māṇavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ tiṟamaiyait terintu koḷvatuṭaṉ, maṟṟavarkaḷ tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārttu, uyāttikkoḷḷa.

4. māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟamai vaḷarcciyai matippīṭu ceyya.

5. āciriyarkaḷ taṅkaḷ pōtaṉaiyil, māṇavarkaḷ evvaḷavu tūram payaṉaṭaintārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷa.

6. mēlum, muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkāka āyvum cōtaṉaiyum mēṟkoḷḷa.

nalla tērvu neṟikkāṉa aḷavu muṟai (Criteria)

1. ettaṉai muṟai tērvukaḷ naṭattiṉālum, orē viḷaivai (Result) ēṟpaṭuttittarakkūṭiya, nampikkaiyai (Reliability) ūṭṭuvatāka tērvu muṟai amaintirukka vēṇṭum.

2. aimpulaṉkaḷālum aṟintu ceyalpaṭuttak kūṭiya tāka (Objectivity) tērvu amaintiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. cikkal illāta eḷiya taṉmai (Simplicity) koṇṭatāka tērvumuṟai irukka vēṇṭum.

4. eṉṟeṉṟum māṟippōkāta nilaiyāṉa taṉmai (Standard) koṇṭatāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

5. tērvukaḷai naṭatta oru cīrāṉa naṭai muṟaikaḷaiyum tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa takavalkaḷaiyum tarak kūṭiyatāka (Standard directions) irukka vēṇṭum.

6. tērvu neṟiyāṉatu etaṉai aḷakkavēṇṭum eṉ patait teḷivāka cuṭṭikkāṭṭi, naṭattum kuṇam koṇ ṭatākavum (Validity) viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

uṭal tiṟat tērvukaḷ (Physical Efficiency Tests)

uṭal tiṟam eṉpatu paṟṟi, pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka āyvukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟālum, oru muṭinta muṭivukku yārālum vara muṭiyavillai .

amerikkāvaic cērnta cārjeṉṭ (Sargent) eṉpavar, tacaivalimaiyāl tāṉ oruvar uṭal tiṟam vaḷarkiṟatu eṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭittuk kūṟiṉār.

tacai valimaiyāṉatu uṭaluṟuppukkaḷiṉ motta ceyal tiṟaṉāl vaḷarkiṟatu eṉṟu piṉṉāḷil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu.

ākavē, oruvar uṭal tiṟam (Physical Fitness) uṭaiyavar eṉpatai, piṉvarum kūṟukaḷ koṇṭu, muṭivu ceyyalām eṉṟa oru marapu ēṟpaṭṭatu.

1. valimai (Strength)

2. vēkam (Speed)

3. nekiḻccit taṉmai (Agility)

4. nīṭittuḻaikkum āṟṟal (Endurance)

5. naramput tacai iṇainta ceyalāṟṟal (Neuro muscular Coordination)

ivaṟṟai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, taṉiyāḷ uṭal tiṟat tērvu, taramāṉa potu uṭal tiṟat tērvu eṉṟu iru pirivukaḷākap pirittu, tērvu ceytu pārkkalām.

taṉiyāḷ uṭal tiṟat tērvu (Individual Physical Efficiency Test)

kīḻkkāṇum uṭaliyakka ceyalkaḷ mūlamāka, paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷiṉ uṭal tiṟaṉai tērvu ceytu pārkkalām.

1. 100 mīṭṭar ōṭṭam (vēkam).

2. uyaram tāṇṭal (nekiḻcciyum, uṟuppukkaḷ iṇainta ceyal āṟṟal)

3. nīḷam tāṇṭal (vēkam, nekiḻcci, uṟuppu ceyal āṟṟal)

4. kirikkeṭ panteṟi (valimai, iṇainta ceyalāṟṟal)

5. toṅku kampiyil ēṟi iṟaṅkal. (valimai, tacaic cakti) (Pull ups)

6. 800 mīṭṭar ōṭṭam (nīṭittuḻaikkum āṟṟal) ovvoru nikaḻccikkum, ivvaḷavu cātaṉai cey tāl, ittaṉai veṟṟi eṇ kiṭaikkum eṉṟu kuṟip piṭṭu, tayārittuk koṇṭu ataṟkup piṟaku, tērvu ceytu, aṟiyalām.

oru mātirip paṭṭiyal

100 mīṭṭar uyaram tāṇṭal nīḷam tāṇṭal kirikkeṭ panteṟi toṅku kampiyil ēṟi iṟaṅkavum 800 mīṭṭar veṟṟi eṇ





2. taramāṉa potu uṭal tiṟat tērvukaḷ (Standard Physical Efficiency Tests)

15-vatu talaippil uḷḷa oṭukaḷappōṭṭikaḷ eṉpatil, tiṟamaṟiyum pōṭṭikaḷ (Tabloid Sports) eṉṟa pakutiyil irukkiṉṟa kuṟippukaḷ, intat tērvuk kum poruntum.





inta miṉṉūlaip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇaiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭaittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇaiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷai varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇaintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷai aṉaivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟaiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻai ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻaikaḷai nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭaippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uraiyai nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:

Fathima Shaila

Pavithra Kannan
